local function fn2(arg, arg2)
	delay(arg2 or 1, function()
		game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Chest.Remotes.Bindables.TextAlert:Fire("Custom Text", {
			Name = "No Force",
			Overlay = true,
			Message = arg or "Zen Hub Loading Script .",
			Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
		})
	end)
end

runAutoAccept = function()
	local tbl = { "EnterTheGame", {} }

	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Chest.Remotes.Functions.EtcFunction:InvokeServer(unpack(tbl))
	end)

	if not ok then
		warn("Failed to execute AutoAccept: ", result)
		return false
	end
	return true
end

local queueOnTeleport = queue_on_teleport or syn and syn.queue_on_teleport or fluxus and fluxus.queue_on_teleport

if queueOnTeleport then
	local queued, err = pcall(queueOnTeleport, "loadstring(game:HttpGet('" .. str .. "'))()")

	if queued then
		print("Auto-exec queued for next teleport")
	else
		warn("[fixed] Failed to queue auto-exec: " .. tostring(err))
	end
else
	warn("Your executor does not support queue_on_teleport")
end

runAutoAccept()
wait(1)

SaveSettings = function()
	local json = HttpService:JSONEncode(Config)

	if writefile then
		if isfolder("ZenHub") then
			if isfolder(str2 .. "/ " .. gameId) then
				writefile(str2 .. "/ " .. gameId .. "/" .. str3, json)
			else
				makefolder(str2 .. "/ " .. gameId .. "/")
				writefile(str2 .. "/ " .. gameId .. "/" .. str3, json)
			end
		else
			makefolder("ZenHub")
			makefolder(str2 .. "/ " .. gameId .. "/")
			writefile(str2 .. "/ " .. gameId .. "/" .. str3, json)
		end
	end
end

LoadSettings = function()
	local HttpService_ = game:GetService("HttpService")

	if isfile(str2 .. "/ " .. gameId .. "/" .. str3) then
		local v3 = pairs
		local data = HttpService_:JSONDecode(readfile(str2 .. "/ " .. gameId .. "/" .. str3)) or Config

		for k, v4 in v3(data) do
			Config[k] = v4
		end
	end
end

bindableFunction.OnInvoke = function(arg)
	if arg == "OK" then
		delfile(str2 .. "/ " .. gameId .. "/" .. str3)
		local TeleportService_ = game:GetService("TeleportService")
		local localPlayer = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer

		if localPlayer then
			TeleportService_:Teleport(game.PlaceId, localPlayer)
		end
	end

	bindableFunction:Destroy()
end

-- [fixed] HttpGet wrapper: Roblox executors throw "ConnectFail" (a.k.a. connectFail)
-- whenever the request cannot leave the client. Previously every call site was
-- unguarded, so a single failed request killed the whole script. This retries a
-- few times and returns nil instead of erroring.
local function safeHttpGet(url, attempts)
	local lastErr

	for _ = 1, (attempts or 3) do
		local ok, res = pcall(function()
			return game:HttpGet(url, true)
		end)

		if ok and type(res) == "string" and #res > 0 then
			return res
		end

		lastErr = res
		task.wait(1)
	end

	warn("[fixed] HttpGet failed: " .. tostring(url) .. " -> " .. tostring(lastErr))

	return nil
end

-- [fixed] Same as safeHttpGet but always hands back a decodable table so the
-- server-list paging loops terminate instead of spinning or erroring.
local function safeServerList(url)
	local body = safeHttpGet(url)

	if body then
		local ok, decoded = pcall(function()
			return HttpService:JSONDecode(body)
		end)

		if ok and type(decoded) == "table" and type(decoded.data) == "table" then
			return decoded
		end
	end

	return { data = {}, nextPageCursor = nil }
end

MonsterList = {}

for k in pairs(monsterPot) do
	local match = k:match("Lv. (%d+)")
	match = match and tonumber(match)

	if match then
		if match >= 1 and match <= 2250 and Sea1 or match >= 2000 and match <= 4000 and Sea2 or match >= 4000 and Sea3 then
			table.insert(MonsterList, k)
		end
	end
end

task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(1.5) do
		pcall(function()
			if Client.Character and Client.Character.Services and Client.Character.Services.Haki and Client.Character.Services.Haki.Value ~= 1 then
				game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Chest.Remotes.Events.Armament:FireServer()
				task.wait(1.5)
			end
		end)
	end
end)

-- [Kingahah] Central noclip control.
-- The original code blindly set CanCollide = false on every BasePart and, on the
-- way out, only destroyed BodyClip. Nothing ever put CanCollide back, so the
-- moment Auto Farm (or any other loop driving tp()) was switched off the
-- character was left with zero collision: it never landed again.
-- We now remember exactly which parts we touched and restore them.
local clippedParts = {}

-- Timestamp of the last tp() call. Any farming loop calls tp() at least once
-- every frame; once it stops for good nothing refreshes this and the clip is
-- released, which is what makes the character drop back to the ground.
_G.NoClipLastUsed = 0

local function restoreCollision()
	for part, wasCollidable in pairs(clippedParts) do
		if part.Parent then
			pcall(function()
				part.CanCollide = wasCollidable
			end)
		end
	end

	table.clear(clippedParts)
end

setNoClip = function(enabled)
	if not enabled then
		_G.NeedNoClip = false
		_G.NoClipLastUsed = 0
		restoreCollision()

		local character = Client and Client.Character

		if character then
			local upperTorso = character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")

			if upperTorso then
				local bodyClip = upperTorso:FindFirstChild("BodyClip")

				if bodyClip then
					bodyClip:Destroy()
				end
			end
		end

		return
	end

	local character = Client and Client.Character

	if not character then
		return
	end

	_G.NeedNoClip = true

	local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

	if humanoid and humanoid.Sit then
		humanoid.Sit = false
	end

	for descendant in pairs(clippedParts) do
		if not descendant.Parent then
			clippedParts[descendant] = nil
		end
	end

	for _, descendant in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
		if descendant:IsA("BasePart") and descendant.CanCollide then
			clippedParts[descendant] = true
			descendant.CanCollide = false
		end
	end

	local upperTorso = character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")

	if not upperTorso then
		return
	end

	local bodyClip = upperTorso:FindFirstChild("BodyClip")

	if not bodyClip then
		local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
		bodyVelocity.Name = "BodyClip"
		bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
		-- original created this with Velocity = (0, 1, 0) and an infinite
		-- MaxForce, which kicked the character straight up on the first frame
		bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
		bodyVelocity.Parent = upperTorso
	else
		bodyClip.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
		bodyClip.Velocity = Vector3.zero
	end
end

task.spawn(function()
	local lastRefresh = 0

	while true do
		task.wait(0.05)

		pcall(function()
			if not _G.NeedNoClip then
				return
			end

			-- nothing has teleported for a while -> whatever was farming is off
			if os.clock() - _G.NoClipLastUsed > 2 then
				setNoClip(false)
				return
			end

			-- re-apply for parts that streamed in since the last pass
			local now = os.clock()

			if now - lastRefresh >= 0.15 then
				lastRefresh = now
				setNoClip(true)
			end
		end)
	end
end)

-- [Kingahah] The script never listened for CharacterAdded, so after a respawn
-- the noclip/spin state and the cached tool names were left pointing at the
-- destroyed character. Reset them so a new body starts clean on the ground.
Client.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
	_G.NeedNoClip = false
	_G.NoClipLastUsed = 0
	restoreCollision()

	if _G.D then
		_G.D:Disconnect()
		_G.D = nil
	end

	if _G.SpinThrust then
		_G.SpinThrust:Destroy()
		_G.SpinThrust = nil
	end

	myWeapon = { Melee = "", Sword = "", Fruit = "" }
	addSkill = "?"
	_G.Weapon = nil

	task.defer(function()
		local rootPart = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)

		if rootPart then
			task.wait(0.5)
			restoreCollision()
		end
	end)
end)

Client.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
	_G.NeedNoClip = false
	_G.NoClipLastUsed = 0
	restoreCollision()
end)

local str4 = nil
local toolTip = nil

-- [Kingahah] Several quest loops run at ~60Hz and used to do
-- `delay(1.5, function() Client.Character.Humanoid.Health = 0 end)` inline. That
-- queued a fresh timer every single frame, so dozens piled up, each of them
-- throwing "index nil with 'Character'" once the character was gone and each
-- re-killing the player right after a respawn. One timer per state, and guarded.
local suicidePending = {}

scheduleSuicide = function(key, seconds)
	if suicidePending[key] then
		return
	end

	suicidePending[key] = true

	delay(seconds or 1.5, function()
		suicidePending[key] = nil

		local char = Client and Client.Character
		local hum = char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

		if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
			hum.Health = 0
		end
	end)
end

getTool = function()
	for _, child in pairs(Client.Character:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("Tool") then
			if child.ToolTip == "Fruit Power" then
				addSkill = "DF"
			end

			str4 = tostring(child.Name)
			toolTip = child.ToolTip
		end
	end

	return str4
end

local tbl = {
	CheckOnCooldown = function(arg)
		getTool()
		local str5 = string.upper(arg) or "Z"
		local skillCooldown = Client.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("SkillCooldown")
		if not skillCooldown then
			return false
		end

		if toolTip == "Sword" then
			local swFrame = skillCooldown:FindFirstChild("SWFrame")
			if not swFrame or not swFrame:FindFirstChild(str5) then
				return true
			end
			local v3 = swFrame[str5]
			if v3.Frame.Frame.Size.X.Scale < 0.73 and not v3:FindFirstChild("Locked").Visible then
				return v3.Frame.Frame.AbsoluteSize.X > 0
			end
		elseif toolTip == "Combat" then
			local fsFrame = skillCooldown:FindFirstChild("FSFrame")
			if not fsFrame or not fsFrame:FindFirstChild(str5) then
				return true
			end
			local v3 = fsFrame[str5]
			if v3.Frame.Frame.Size.X.Scale < 0.73 and not v3:FindFirstChild("Locked").Visible then
				return v3.Frame.Frame.AbsoluteSize.X > 0
			end
		elseif toolTip == "Fruit Power" then
			local dfFrame = skillCooldown:FindFirstChild("DFFrame")
			if not dfFrame or not dfFrame:FindFirstChild(str5) then
				return true
			end
			local v3 = dfFrame[str5]
			if v3.Frame.Frame.Size.X.Scale < 0.73 and not v3:FindFirstChild("Locked").Visible then
				return v3.Frame.Frame.AbsoluteSize.X > 0
			end
		end

		return false
	end,
	CheckSkillLock = function(arg)
		getTool()
		local str5 = string.upper(arg) or "Z"
		local skillCooldown = Client.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("SkillCooldown")
		if not skillCooldown then
			return true
		end

		if toolTip == "Sword" then
			local swFrame = skillCooldown:FindFirstChild("SWFrame")
			if not swFrame or not swFrame:FindFirstChild(str5) then
				return true
			end

			if not swFrame[str5]:FindFirstChild("Locked").Visible then
				return false
			end
		elseif toolTip == "Combat" then
			local fsFrame = skillCooldown:FindFirstChild("FSFrame")
			if not fsFrame or not fsFrame:FindFirstChild(str5) then
				return true
			end

			if not fsFrame[str5]:FindFirstChild("Locked").Visible then
				return false
			end
		elseif toolTip == "Fruit Power" then
			local dfFrame = skillCooldown:FindFirstChild("DFFrame")
			if not dfFrame or not dfFrame:FindFirstChild(str5) then
				return true
			end

			if not dfFrame[str5]:FindFirstChild("Locked").Visible then
				return false
			end
		end

		return true
	end,
}

local function fn4()
	local character = Client.Character
	if not character or not character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
		return nil, 0
	end
	local position = character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
	local n = 0
	local v3 = nil

	for _, descendant in ipairs(workspace.Effects:GetDescendants()) do
		local position2

		if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
			position2 = descendant.Position
		else
			position2 = nil

			if descendant:IsA("Model") then
				local ok, result = pcall(function()
					return descendant:GetPivot()
				end)

				position2 = nil

				if ok then
					position2 = result.Position
				end
			end
		end

		if position2 then
			local magnitude = (position2 - position).Magnitude

			if n < magnitude then
				v3 = descendant
				n = magnitude
			end
		end
	end

	return v3, n
end

tp = function(arg)
	if not Client.Character or Client.Character.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
		return
	end
	local character = Client.Character
	local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not humanoidRootPart then
		return
	end
	local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

	if humanoid and humanoid.Sit then
		humanoid.Sit = false
	end

	-- [Kingahah] routed through setNoClip so the parts we disable can be
	-- restored later, and so the watchdog below knows farming is still active
	setNoClip(true)
	_G.NoClipLastUsed = os.clock()

	local v3

	-- [Kingahah] fn4() walks every descendant of workspace.Effects, and tp()
	-- runs once per frame per farm loop. Calling it unconditionally made the
	-- scan happen ~20x/second for nothing; only the dungeon branch uses it.
	local target = arg.Target or nil
	local mod = arg.Mod or CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)
	if not target then
		warn("Target CFrame is missing.")
		return
	end

	if Config["Auto New Clear Dungeon"] then
		v3 = fn4()

		local flag = false

		if v3 then
			if v3:IsA("BasePart") then
				if dist(v3.Position) < 300 then
					flag = true
				end
			elseif v3:IsA("Model") then
				local ok, result = pcall(function()
					return v3:GetPivot()
				end)

				if ok and dist(result.Position) < 300 then
					flag = true
				end
			end
		end

		if flag and v3 then
			local cFrame

			if v3:IsA("BasePart") then
				cFrame = v3.CFrame
			else
				cFrame = nil

				if v3:IsA("Model") then
					local ok

					ok, cFrame = pcall(function()
						return v3:GetPivot()
					end)

					local v4 = nil

					if not ok then
						cFrame = v4
					end
				end
			end

			if cFrame then
				humanoidRootPart.CFrame = cFrame * CFrame.new(-300, 0, 0)
				return
			end
		end
	end

	humanoidRootPart.CFrame = target * mod
end

sendwebhook = function(arg, arg2)
	local HttpService_ = game:GetService("HttpService")

	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return HttpService_:JSONEncode(arg2)
	end)

	if not ok then
		print("Error encoding data to JSON:", result)
		return
	end
	local tbl2 = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json" }

	pcall(function()
		if type(request) ~= "function" then
			warn("[fixed] No request function available, skipping webhook")
			return
		end

		local posted, postErr = pcall(request, { Url = arg, Body = result, Method = "POST", Headers = tbl2 })

		if not posted then
			warn("[fixed] Webhook POST failed: " .. tostring(postErr))
		end

		return posted
	end)

	return successF
end

EquipTools = function(arg)
	if Client.Backpack:FindFirstChild(arg) then
		local v3 = Client.Backpack:FindFirstChild(arg)
		Client.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(v3)
	end
end

UnEquipTools = function(arg)
	local v3 = Client.Character:FindFirstChild(arg)

	if v3 then
		task.wait(0.5)
		v3.Parent = Client.Backpack
		task.wait(0.1)
	end
end

Attack = function()
	if Client.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("EatFruitBecky") and Config["Auto Store Fruit"] then
		return
	end

	if Config["Select Weapon"] == "Melee" then
		addSkill = "FS"
		Repli.Chest.Remotes.Functions.SkillAction:InvokeServer("FS_" .. _G.Weapon .. "_M1")
		EquipTools(_G.Weapon)
	elseif Config["Select Weapon"] == "Sword" then
		addSkill = "SW"
		Repli.Chest.Remotes.Functions.SkillAction:InvokeServer("SW_" .. _G.Weapon .. "_M1")
	elseif Config["Select Weapon"] == "Melee&Sword" then
		addSkill = "SW"

		delay(0.1, function()
			Repli.Chest.Remotes.Functions.SkillAction:InvokeServer("SW_" .. myWeapon.Sword .. "_M1")
		end)

		Repli.Chest.Remotes.Functions.SkillAction:InvokeServer("FS_" .. myWeapon.Melee .. "_M1")
		EquipTools(myWeapon.Sword)
	elseif Config["Select Weapon"] == "Fruit" then
		EquipTools(myWeapon.Fruit)
	elseif Config["Select Weapon"] == "Melee&Fruit" then
		Repli.Chest.Remotes.Functions.SkillAction:InvokeServer("FS_" .. myWeapon.Melee .. "_M1")
		EquipTools(myWeapon.Fruit)
	elseif Config["Select Weapon"] == "Sword&Fruit" then
		local sword = tick() % 1 < 0.5 and myWeapon.Sword or myWeapon.Fruit

		if sword and sword ~= "" then
			EquipTools(sword)
		end

		if myWeapon.Sword and myWeapon.Sword ~= "" then
			Repli.Chest.Remotes.Functions.SkillAction:InvokeServer("SW_" .. myWeapon.Sword .. "_M1")
		end

		if myWeapon.Fruit and myWeapon.Fruit ~= "" then
			Repli.Chest.Remotes.Functions.SkillAction:InvokeServer("DF_" .. myWeapon.Fruit .. "_M1")
		end
	elseif Config["Select Weapon"] == "All for One" then
		local n = tick() % 1.5
		local melee

		if n < 0.5 then
			melee = myWeapon.Melee
		elseif n < 1 then
			melee = myWeapon.Sword
		else
			melee = myWeapon.Fruit
		end

		if melee and melee ~= "" then
			EquipTools(melee)
		end

		if myWeapon.Melee and myWeapon.Melee ~= "" then
			Repli.Chest.Remotes.Functions.SkillAction:InvokeServer("FS_" .. myWeapon.Melee .. "_M1")
		end

		if myWeapon.Sword and myWeapon.Sword ~= "" then
			Repli.Chest.Remotes.Functions.SkillAction:InvokeServer("SW_" .. myWeapon.Sword .. "_M1")
		end

		if myWeapon.Fruit and myWeapon.Fruit ~= "" then
			Repli.Chest.Remotes.Functions.SkillAction:InvokeServer("DF_" .. myWeapon.Fruit .. "_M1")
		end
	else
		Repli.Chest.Remotes.Functions.SkillAction:InvokeServer("FS_" .. _G.Weapon .. "_M1")
	end

	if Config["Select Weapon"] == nil and Config["Select Weapon"] ~= "Melee&Sword" and Config["Select Weapon"] ~= "Sword&Fruit" and Config["Select Weapon"] ~= "All for One" then
		EquipTools(_G.Weapon)
	end

	if myWeapon.Fruit == "DragonDragon" then
		game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Chest"):WaitForChild("Remotes"):WaitForChild("Functions"):WaitForChild("SkillAction"):InvokeServer("DF_DragonDragon_M1")
	end

	local selectWeapon = Config["Select Weapon"]

	if selectWeapon ~= "Sword&Fruit" and selectWeapon ~= "All for One" then
		local tool = Client.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")

		if tool then
			tool.Parent = Client.LocalPlayer.Backpack
		end
	end

	if _G.Debug then
		warn("[DEBUG]", "NOW ATTACK")
	end
end

UseSkillRemote = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
	local tool = Client.Character and Client.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
	if not tool then
		return
	end
	-- [Kingahah] was `Client.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").CFrame`,
	-- which throws while the body is still loading
	if not arg2 then
		local rootPart = Client.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not rootPart then
			return
		end

		arg2 = rootPart.CFrame
	end

	local str5

	if tool.ToolTip == "Fruit Power" then
		str5 = "DF"
	else
		str5 = "SW"

		if tool.ToolTip == "Combat" then
			str5 = "FS"
		end
	end

	Repli.Chest.Remotes.Functions.SkillAction:InvokeServer(str5 .. "_" .. tool.Name .. "_" .. arg, { Type = "Up", MouseHit = arg2 * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, 0) })
	task.wait(arg3 or 0)
	Repli.Chest.Remotes.Functions.SkillAction:InvokeServer(str5 .. "_" .. tool.Name .. "_" .. arg, { Type = "Down", MouseHit = arg2 * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, 0) })
end

useSkill = function()
	if Config["Auto Use Skill"] and Client.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
		for _, v3 in next, Config["Select Skill"], nil do
			if v3 == "V" and Client.Character:FindFirstChild("Dragon") then
				return
			end

			if type(v3) == "string" and not tbl.CheckOnCooldown(v3) and not tbl.CheckSkillLock(v3) then
				game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(true, v3, false, game)
				game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(false, v3, false, game)
			end
		end
	end
end

click = function(arg)
	local n = arg or 3

	for i = 1, n do
		game:GetService("VirtualUser"):Button1Down(Vector2.new(1, 1))
		game:GetService("VirtualUser"):Button1Up(Vector2.new(1, 1))
	end
end

dist = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
	-- [Kingahah] `(Client and Client.Character).HumanoidRootPart` throws whenever
	-- the player has no body (dead / respawning), which killed whichever farm
	-- loop was mid-distance-check. Treat "no position" as infinitely far.
	if not arg2 then
		local char = Client and Client.Character
		local rootPart = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not rootPart then
			return math.huge
		end

		arg2 = rootPart.Position
	end

	local vector = Vector3.new
	local x = arg.X
	local flag = not arg3
	-- [Kingahah] `flag and arg.Y` evaluates to the *boolean* false when the 2D
	-- mode is on, and Vector3.new(x, false, z) throws. Coerce properly.
	local y = flag and arg.Y or 0
	local y2 = flag and arg2.Y or 0
	return (vector(x, y, arg.Z) - Vector3.new(arg2.X, y2, arg2.Z)).magnitude
end

local function fn5(...)
	local tbl2 = { ... }
	local position = tbl2[1]

	if type(position) ~= "vector" then
		if type(position) == "userdata" then
			position = position.Position
		else
			local kind = type(position)
			position = nil

			if kind == "number" then
				position = CFrame.new(unpack(tbl2)).p
			end
		end
	end

	local huge = math.huge
	local name = nil

	for _, child in pairs(workspace.Island:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("Model") then
			local magnitude = (position - child:GetModelCFrame().p).Magnitude

			if magnitude < huge then
				name = child.Name
				huge = magnitude
			end
		end
	end

	if name then
		return name
	end
end

local tbl2 = {}

local function fn6(arg)
	for _, child in pairs(workspace.AllNPC:GetChildren()) do
		local v3 = fn5(child.CFrame)

		if v3 == fn5(arg) then
			if not tbl2[v3] then
				tbl2[v3] = {}
			end

			table.insert(tbl2[v3], child.CFrame)
		end
	end
end

local function fn7(arg, arg2)
	if arg.LevelRequired == 3250 then
		tp({
			Target = CFrame.new(-1383.8770751953125, 202.27455139160156, 8899.5986328125) * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0),
		})
	elseif arg.LevelRequired == 3750 then
		tp({ Target = workspace.Island["H - Fiore"].Lab.Lab.Base.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0) })
	elseif arg.LevelRequired == 3775 then
		tp({
			Target = workspace.Island["H - Fiore"].Italian.Base.Mountain.Model:GetChildren()[9].CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0),
		})
	elseif arg.LevelRequired == 4750 then
		tp({
			Target = workspace.Island["Forgotten Coliseum"].Vacuus.Base:GetChildren()[179].CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0),
		})
	else
		fn6(arg2)
		local mob = arg.Mob
		loadIslandForAllNPCs(fn5(arg2), mob)
	end
end

local function fn8(arg)
	local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")

	-- [Kingahah] the distance was computed from Client.Character *above* the guard
	-- that checks it exists, so every quest teleport while dead/respawning threw
	-- before the guard could warn
	if not (Client and Client.Character and Client.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")) then
		warn("HumanoidRootPart not found on Client's character")
		return
	end

	local magnitude = (arg.Position - Client.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
	local tween = TweenService:Create(Client.Character.HumanoidRootPart, TweenInfo.new(magnitude / 9000, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { CFrame = arg })
	tween:Play()
	tween.Completed:Wait()
end

TackTime = tick()

getQuestOld = function(cFrame, arg, arg2)
	if not arg2 then
	end

	if cFrame == nil then
		if Sea1 then
			cFrame = "Elite Pirate"
		elseif Sea3 then
			cFrame = "The Squid"
		end
	end

	if arg == 1 and Sea2 then
		cFrame = "Elite Pirate"
	end

	if arg == 3 and Sea2 then
		cFrame = "The Squid"
	end

	if cFrame == "Elite Pirate" then
		cFrame = workspace.AllNPC:FindFirstChild("Elite Pirate").CFrame
	elseif cFrame == "The Squid" then
		cFrame = workspace.AllNPC:FindFirstChild("The Squid").CFrame
	end

	fn8(cFrame)
	Client.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").CFrame = cFrame

	local function fn9()
		for _, child in pairs(workspace.AllNPC:GetChildren()) do
			if child and (child.Position - cFrame.Position).Magnitude < 5 then
				return child
			end
		end

		return nil
	end

	local function fn10(arg3)
		for k in pairs(require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Chest.Modules.ChatDialogue)) do
			if k == arg3 then
				return true
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	local checkQuest = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Chest"):WaitForChild("Remotes"):WaitForChild("Functions"):WaitForChild("CheckQuest")
	local invokeServer = checkQuest.InvokeServer
	local v3 = fn9()
	invokeServer(checkQuest, v3)
	local flag = false

	if fn10(fn9().Name) then
		game:GetService("VirtualUser"):Button1Down(Vector2.new(1, 1))
		game:GetService("VirtualUser"):Button1Up(Vector2.new(1, 1))
		flag = true
	end

	if Client.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Elite Pirate") then
		if arg == 1 or arg == 2 then
			arg = "Confirm"
		elseif arg == 3 then
			arg = "Sea 3"
		end
	elseif Client.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("The Squid") then
		if arg == 1 and Sea3 then
			arg = "Sea 1"
		elseif arg == 2 or arg == 1 and Sea2 or Sea2 and arg == 3 then
			arg = "Confirm"
		end
	end

	if not cFrame then
		warn("Position not found")
		return
	end

	local function fn11()
		for k in pairs(Client.PlayerGui:GetChildren()) do
			for _, child in pairs(game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:GetChildren()) do
				if flag and child.Name == "Main" then
					for _, child2 in pairs(child:GetChildren()) do
						if child2.Name ~= "Dialogue" then
							continue
						end

						if child2:FindFirstChild(arg or "Button1") and child2.Size.X.Scale > 0 then
							return child2[arg or "Button1"]
						end
					end

					continue
				end

				if child:FindFirstChild("Dialogue") then
					if child.Dialogue:FindFirstChild(arg or "Accept") and not child.Dialogue:FindFirstChild("ContinueLabel") then
						return child.Dialogue[arg or "Accept"]
					end
					local VirtualInputManager2 = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
					VirtualInputManager2:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 0)
					VirtualInputManager2:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 0)
				end
			end
		end

		return nil
	end

	local v4 = fn11()

	if v4 then
		GuiService.SelectedObject = v4
		VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, "Return", false, game)
		VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, "Return", false, game)
		wait(0.5)
		GuiService.SelectedObject = nil
	end
end

noobExechop = function()
	while true do
		local Players_ = game:GetService("Players")
		local GuiService2 = game:GetService("GuiService")
		local VirtualInputManager2 = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
		local playerGui = Players_.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui
		local serverBrowserFrame = playerGui.MainGui.StarterFrame.ServerBrowserFrame
		local serverFrame = serverBrowserFrame.ServerFrame

		if not serverBrowserFrame.Visible then
			GuiService2.SelectedObject = playerGui.TopbarPlus.TopbarContainer.ServerBrowser.IconButton
			VirtualInputManager2:SendKeyEvent(true, "Return", false, game)
			VirtualInputManager2:SendKeyEvent(false, "Return", false, game)
		else
			local children = serverFrame:GetChildren()

			if #children == 1 then
				GuiService2.SelectedObject = serverBrowserFrame.RefreshButton
				VirtualInputManager2:SendKeyEvent(true, "Return", false, game)
				VirtualInputManager2:SendKeyEvent(false, "Return", false, game)
			else
				GuiService2.SelectedObject = children[math.random(2, #children - 1)].JoinButton
				VirtualInputManager2:SendKeyEvent(true, "Return", false, game)
				VirtualInputManager2:SendKeyEvent(false, "Return", false, game)
			end
		end

		wait()
		if game.PlaceId == game.PlaceId then
			continue
		end
		break
	end
end

Serverhop = function()
	local HttpService_ = game:GetService("HttpService")
	local placeId = game.PlaceId
	local str5 = "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. placeId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"

	ListServers = function(arg)
		return safeServerList(str5 .. (arg and "&cursor=" .. arg or ""))
	end

	local nextPageCursor = nil
	local v3
	local tries = 0

	-- [Kingahah] this was a bare `repeat ... until v3` with no attempt cap and no
	-- yield: if the fetch failed (or the list came back empty) it spun as fast
	-- as the thread could schedule, hammering games.roblox.com and freezing the
	-- client. Capped, paced, and gives up cleanly.
	repeat
		tries = tries + 1

		if tries > 10 then
			break
		end

		task.wait(1)

		local v4 = ListServers(nextPageCursor)
		v3 = v4.data[1]
		nextPageCursor = v4.nextPageCursor
	until v3 or not nextPageCursor

	if not v3 then
		warn("[fixed] Serverhop aborted: no server list available")
		return
	end

	game:GetService("TeleportService"):TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId, v3.id, game.Players.LocalPlayer)
end

HopServer = function()
	spawn(Serverhop)
end

local obj = setmetatable({}, {})

-- [Kingahah] These used to return the *string* "nil" instead of nil. That made
-- every `if obj[name] then` guard truthy, so each toggle without an explicit
-- callback did `task.spawn("nil", value)` -> "attempt to call a string" on
-- every single click, and any typo'd obj.Entity call silently became a string
-- error instead of a no-op.
obj.__index = function()
	return nil
end

obj.Entity = setmetatable({}, {})

obj.Entity.__index = function()
	return nil
end

obj.getPlayerMaterial = function(arg)
	for k, v3 in pairs(game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(Client.PlayerStats.Material.Value)) do
		if k == arg then
			return v3
		end
	end

	return 0
end

local RaidBossList = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Chest.Modules.RaidBossList)

Isboss = function(arg)
	for k in pairs(RaidBossList) do
		if k == arg then
			return true
		end
	end
end

obj.GetQuestData = function(arg, arg2)
	local tbl3 = {}
	local value = Client.PlayerStats.lvl.Value

	for k, v3 in pairs(require(QuestManager)) do
		if not v3.DailyQuest then
			tonumber(v3.Mob:match("Lv%. (%d+)"))

			if v3.Mob:match("Lv") and value >= v3.Level then
				local insert = table.insert
				local tbl4 = { LevelRequired = v3.Level or 1 }

				local function fn9()
					if value >= 3300 and value < 3375 then
						return "Azlan [Lv. 3300]"
					end
					return v3.Mob
				end

				tbl4.Mob = fn9()

				local function fn10()
					if value >= 3300 and value < 3375 then
						return "Kill 4 Azlan"
					end
					return k
				end

				tbl4.QuestTitle = fn10()

				local function fn11()
					local tbl5 = {}
					local v4 = pairs
					local allNPC = game:GetService("Workspace"):FindFirstChild("AllNPC")

					for _, child in v4(allNPC:GetChildren()) do
						if child:GetAttribute("LevelMin") then
							local level = v3.Level

							if child:GetAttribute("LevelMin") <= level then
								tbl5[#tbl5 + 1] = { Level = child:GetAttribute("LevelMin"), CFrame = child.CFrame }
							end
						end
					end

					table.sort(tbl5, function(arg3, arg4)
						return arg3.Level > arg4.Level
					end)

					return tbl5[1]
				end

				tbl4.NPC = fn11()
				insert(tbl3, tbl4)
			end
		end
	end

	local function fn9()
		table.sort(tbl3, function(arg3, arg4)
			return arg3.LevelRequired > arg4.LevelRequired
		end)

		return tbl3
	end

	tbl3 = fn9()

	-- [Kingahah] tbl3 can legitimately come back empty (every QuestManager entry
	-- filtered out by the level check), and then `tbl3[1].Mob` threw, taking the
	-- whole Auto Farm Level tick down with it.
	if type(tbl3) ~= "table" or #tbl3 == 0 then
		return nil
	end

	if arg2 then
		if Isboss(tbl3[1].Mob) then
			for i = 1, #tbl3 do
				if not Isboss(tbl3[i].Mob) then
					tbl3 = tbl3[i]
					break
				end
			end
		else
			tbl3 = tbl3[1]
		end
	else
		tbl3 = tbl3[1]
	end

	if value >= MaxLevelOfSea and Sea2 then
		tbl3.Mob = "Ryu [Lv. 3975]"
		tbl3.LevelRequired = 3950
		tbl3.QuestTitle = "Kill 1 Ryu"
	elseif value >= MaxLevelOfSea and Sea1 then
		tbl3.Mob = "Seasoned Fishman [Lv. 2200]"
		tbl3.LevelRequired = 2200
		tbl3.QuestTitle = "Kill 1 Seasoned Fishman"
	elseif value >= 4900 and value < 5000 and Sea3 then
		tbl3.Mob = "Prisoner of Gravity [Lv. 4875]"
		tbl3.LevelRequired = 4875
		tbl3.QuestTitle = "Kill 1 Prisoner of Gravity"
	end

	for k, v3 in pairs(QuestMaterial) do
		if tbl3.LevelRequired == tonumber(k) then
			local flag = nil

			for _, child in pairs(workspace.Monster.Mon:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name == tbl3.Mob and child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and child.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
					flag = true
				end
			end

			for _, child in pairs(workspace.Monster.Boss:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name == tbl3.Mob and child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and child.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
					flag = true
				end
			end

			for _, child in pairs(Repli.MOB:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name == tbl3.Mob and child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and child.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
					flag = true
				end
			end

			if obj.getPlayerMaterial(v3.Material) <= 0 and not flag then
				tbl3.Mob = v3.Kills
				tbl3.LevelRequired = v3.Level
				tbl3.QuestTitle = v3.QuestTitle
			elseif obj.getPlayerMaterial(v3.Material) > 0 and not flag then
				local tbl4 = {
					"QuestSpawnBoss",
					{
						SuccessQuest = "Quest Accepted.",
						BossName = tbl3.Mob,
						LevelNeed = tbl3.LevelRequired,
						QuestName = tbl3.QuestTitle,
						MaterialNeed = v3.Material,
					},
				}

				Repli:WaitForChild("Chest"):WaitForChild("Remotes"):WaitForChild("Functions"):WaitForChild("EtcFunction"):InvokeServer(unpack(tbl4))
			end
		end
	end

	return tbl3
end

local v3 = pairs
local allNPC = game:GetService("Workspace"):FindFirstChild("AllNPC")

for _, child in v3(allNPC:GetChildren()) do
	if child:GetAttribute("LevelMax") then
		lvmax = child:GetAttribute("LevelMax")

		if not MaxLevelOfSea or lvmax > MaxLevelOfSea then
			MaxLevelOfSea = lvmax
		end
	end

	if child:GetAttribute("LevelMin") then
		lvmin = child:GetAttribute("LevelMin")

		if not MinLevelOfSea or lvmin < MinLevelOfSea then
			-- [Kingahah] was `MinLevelOfSea = lvmax`, a copy-paste slip
			MinLevelOfSea = lvmin
		end
	end
end

if InRaid then
	MaxLevelOfSea = 4800
end

local tbl3 = { "Boss", "Minion" }
local tbl4 = {}

obj.Entity.Bring = function()
end

UpdateStandMethond = function()
	if Config.FlightAt == "Above" then
		Config.standmethod = CFrame.new(0, Config.RawStand or 8.5, 0.15) * CFrame.Angles(-1.5707963267948966, 0, 0)
	elseif Config.FlightAt == "Beside" then
		Config.standmethod = CFrame.new(0, 0, Config.RawStand)
	elseif Config.FlightAt == "Below" then
		-- [Kingahah] `-Config.RawStand or 8.5` parses as `(-RawStand) or 8.5`,
		-- so the fallback never applied and a nil RawStand would have thrown
		Config.standmethod = CFrame.new(0, -(Config.RawStand or 8.5), -1) * CFrame.Angles(1.5707963267948966, 0, 0)
	else
		Config.standmethod = CFrame.new(0, Config.RawStand or 8.5, 0.15) * CFrame.Angles(-1.5707963267948966, 0, 0)
	end
end

UpdateStandMethond()

comma_value = function(arg)
	local v4

	while true do
		local v5
		v4, v5 = string.gsub(arg, "^(-?%d+)(%d%d%d)", "%1,%2")

		if v5 == 0 then
			break
		else
			arg = v4
		end
	end

	return v4
end

local flag = false

updateLabel = function(arg, arg2)
	if arg and arg:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
		local health = arg.Humanoid.Health
		local maxHealth = arg.Humanoid.MaxHealth
		local n = maxHealth > 0 and math.floor(health / maxHealth * 100) or 0
		local str5 = health > 0 and "Alive" or "Dead"
		_G.LabelAutoFarm = "Farming: " .. arg.Name
		_G.Questname = "Quest: " .. (arg2 and arg2.QuestTitle or "None")
		_G.LabelHealth = "Status: " .. str5 .. " | Health: " .. n .. "%"
	else
		_G.LabelAutoFarm = "No target detected"
		_G.LabelHealth = ""
	end
end

obj.Entity.attack = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
	-- [Kingahah] this list used to be one chunk-level table shared by attack(),
	-- "Auto Kill Jack o lantern" and "Auto New Clear Dungeon". The first loop to
	-- finish ran table.clear() + task.cancel over everybody else's live attack
	-- threads, so a second toggle would silently stop attacking. Per invocation.
	local tbl5 = {}
	local v4

	if Config["Not Accept Boss"] then
		v4 = obj.GetQuestData(nil, true)
	else
		v4 = obj.GetQuestData()
	end

	local children, children2

	if Sea2 then
		children = game:GetService("Workspace").SeaMonster:GetChildren()
		-- [Kingahah] GhostMonster only exists while a Ghost Ship event is running, so
		-- this threw in Sea 2 at every other time and killed every Sea2 farm loop
		local ghostFolder = game.Workspace:FindFirstChild("GhostMonster")
		children2 = ghostFolder and ghostFolder:GetChildren() or {}
	elseif Sea3 then
		children = game:GetService("Workspace").SeaMonster:GetChildren()
		children2 = Repli.MOB:GetChildren()
	else
		children = Repli.MOB:GetChildren()
		children2 = Repli.MOB:GetChildren()
	end

	local function fn9(arg5)
		if arg2 == "Auto Farm Level" and Client.CurrentQuest.Value ~= v4.QuestTitle or not Config[arg2] then
			return
		end

		if arg5.Name == "Tentacle" then
			if not arg5:FindFirstChild("Next") then
				while true do
					task.wait()

					if arg5:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and arg5:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and arg5.Humanoid.Health > 0 and arg5.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Y > -100 and arg5.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Y < 1500 then
						if arg3 and type(arg3) == "number" and arg3 > 1 then
							tp({
								Target = arg5.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, arg3 or 8.5, 0.15) * CFrame.Angles(-1.5707963267948966, 0, 0),
							})
						elseif game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("EatFruitBecky") then
							tp({
								Target = arg5.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 500, 100) * CFrame.Angles(-1.5707963267948966, 0, 0),
							})
						else
							tp({ Target = arg5.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * Config.standmethod })
						end

						updateLabel(arg5, v4)

						local function fn10()
							Attack()
							local cFrame = arg5.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
							getgenv().PosMonSkill = cFrame
							useSkill()
						end

						tbl5[#tbl5 + 1] = task.spawn(pcall, fn10)

						-- [Kingahah] this sits inside `while true do task.wait()`, so it fired ~60x a
					-- second: every tick created a "Next" Folder on the mob, and the
					-- very first one immediately terminated the fight loop below.
					-- One marker per fight, and only if it is not already there.
					if not arg5:FindFirstChild("Next") then
						task.spawn(function()
							local folder = Instance.new("Folder", arg5)
							folder.Name = "Next"

							task.wait(25)

							if folder.Parent then
								folder:Destroy()
							end
						end)
					end
				end

					if not (arg5:FindFirstChild("Next") or not Config[arg2] or not arg5.Parent or arg5.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not arg5:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or arg5:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").Position.Y < -100 or arg5:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").Position.Y > 1500) then
						continue
					end
					break
				end

				if #tbl5 >= 1 then
					for _, v5 in pairs(tbl5) do
						pcall(task.cancel, v5)
					end

					table.clear(tbl5)
				end
			end
		else
			local humanoidRootPart = arg5.HumanoidRootPart

			while true do
				task.wait()
				local n = Client.Character.Humanoid.Health / Client.Character.Humanoid.MaxHealth * 100

				if arg5:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and arg5:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and arg5.Humanoid.Health > 0 and arg5.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Y > -100 and arg5.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Y < 1500 and Client.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
					updateLabel(arg5, v4)

					if not Config["Safe Mode"] then
						local function fn10()
							if Config.Bring_Mon then
								obj.Entity.Bring(arg5, humanoidRootPart)
							end
						end

						if arg5.Parent == workspace.Monster.Mon then
							if Config["Instance Damage"] and not table.find(tbl3, arg5.Name) and arg5:FindFirstChild("Head") and arg5:FindFirstChild("Humanoid").Health / arg5:FindFirstChild("Humanoid").MaxHealth * 100 <= 80 then
								obj["Instance Damage"](arg5)
							end
						elseif arg5.Name == "Dragon [Lv. 5000]" then
							if Config["Instance Damage"] and not table.find(tbl3, arg5.Name) and arg5:FindFirstChild("Head") and arg5:FindFirstChild("Humanoid").Health / arg5:FindFirstChild("Humanoid").MaxHealth * 100 <= 80 then
								obj["Instance Damage"](arg5)
							end
						end

						tbl5[#tbl5 + 1] = task.spawn(pcall, fn10)

						if arg3 and arg3 > 1 then
							tp({
								Target = arg5.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, arg3 or 8.5, 0.15) * CFrame.Angles(-1.5707963267948966, 0, 0),
							})
						elseif game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("EatFruitBecky") then
							tp({
								Target = arg5.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 500, 100) * CFrame.Angles(-1.5707963267948966, 0, 0),
							})
						else
							tp({ Target = arg5.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * Config.standmethod })
						end

						if _G.Debug then
							warn("[DEBUG]", Config.standmethod, "standmethod")
						end

						if arg5.Parent == workspace.Monster.Mon then
							local health = Client.Character.Humanoid.Health

							delay(1, function()
								if health == Client.Character.Humanoid.Health and arg5:FindFirstChild("Head") then
									obj["Instance Damage"](arg5)
								end
							end)
						end

						local function fn11()
							local cFrame = arg5.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
							getgenv().PosMonSkill = cFrame
							useSkill()
							Attack()
						end

						tbl5[#tbl5 + 1] = task.spawn(pcall, fn11)
						NeedAttack = true
					elseif n <= 50 then
						if arg5:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
							tp({ Target = arg5.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 200, 0) })
						end

						while true do
							task.wait()
							local n2 = Client.Character.Humanoid.Health / Client.Character.Humanoid.MaxHealth * 100
							local flag2 = not Config[arg2]

							if not flag2 then
								flag2 = n2 > (Config["Select You HP Max (%)"] or 75)
							end

							if not flag2 then
								continue
							end
							break
						end
					elseif Config["Select You HP (%)"] < n then
						local function fn10()
							if Config.Bring_Mon then
								obj.Entity.Bring(arg5, humanoidRootPart)
							end
						end

						tbl5[#tbl5 + 1] = task.spawn(pcall, fn10)
						tp({ Target = arg5.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * Config.standmethod })

						local function fn11()
							local cFrame = arg5.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
							getgenv().PosMonSkill = cFrame
							useSkill()
							Attack()
						end

						tbl5[#tbl5 + 1] = task.spawn(pcall, fn11)
						NeedAttack = true
					else
						tp({ Target = workspace.SpawnPoints.Spawn1.CFrame })

						if Client.Character:FindFirstChild("Cyborg") or Client.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Cyborg") then
							while true do
								local n2 = Client.Character.Humanoid.Health / Client.Character.Humanoid.MaxHealth * 100

								if not Client.Character:FindFirstChild("Cyborg") then
									EquipTools("Cyborg")
								end

								if Client.Character:FindFirstChild("Cyborg") and not tbl.CheckOnCooldown("E") and not tbl.CheckSkillLock("E") then
									game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(true, "E", false, game)
								end

								if Client.Character:FindFirstChild("Cyborg") and tbl.CheckOnCooldown("E") and not tbl.CheckSkillLock("E") then
									game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(false, "E", false, game)
								end

								task.wait()
								local flag2 = not Config[arg2]

								if not flag2 then
									flag2 = n2 > (Config["Select You HP Max (%)"] or 75)
								end

								if not flag2 then
									continue
								end
								break
							end
						else
							while true do
								task.wait()
								local n2 = Client.Character.Humanoid.Health / Client.Character.Humanoid.MaxHealth * 100
								local flag2 = not Config[arg2]

								if not flag2 then
									flag2 = n2 > (Config["Select You HP Max (%)"] or 75)
								end

								if not flag2 then
									continue
								end
								break
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if not (arg2 == "Auto Farm Level" and not flag or arg2 == "Auto Farm Level" and Client.CurrentQuest.Value ~= v4.QuestTitle or arg4 and arg4.Monster and arg4.get == "have" and obj.Entity.find(arg4.Monster) or arg4 and arg4.Monster and arg4.get == "not" and not obj.Entity.find(arg4.Monster) or not Config[arg2] or not arg5.Parent or arg5.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or Client.Character.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not arg5:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or arg5:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").Position.Y < -100 or arg5:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").Position.Y > 1500) then
					continue
				end
				break
			end

			if #tbl5 >= 1 then
				for _, v5 in pairs(tbl5) do
					pcall(task.cancel, v5)
				end

				table.clear(tbl5)
			end

			if tbl4[arg5.Name] then
				table.clear(tbl4)
			end
		end
	end

	local v5 = ipairs
	local tbl6 = {}
	local children3 = workspace.Monster.Boss:GetChildren()
	local children4 = workspace.Monster.Mon:GetChildren()
	local mob = Repli.MOB
	local getChildren = mob.GetChildren
	tbl6[1] = children3
	tbl6[2] = children4
	tbl6[3] = children2
	tbl6[4] = children

	do
		local values = table.pack(getChildren(mob))
		table.move(values, 1, values.n, 5, tbl6)
	end

	for _, v6 in v5(tbl6) do
		for _, v7 in ipairs(v6) do
			if table.find(arg, v7.Name) then
				if Config[arg2] then
					fn9(v7)
				else
					-- [Kingahah] The original did `fn9(v7); continue` here, so this
					-- loop never exited while the mob stayed alive: a busy spin
					-- that ran forever with farming switched off. `continue` was
					-- meant to be "not ready yet", so drop the no-op fn9() call
					-- and just break out once the mob is gone.
					while true do
						task.wait()

						local ready = v7.Parent
							and v7:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
							and v7.Humanoid.Health > 0
							and v7:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							and v7.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Y >= -100

						if not ready then
							break
						end
					end
				end
			elseif v6 == Repli.MOB:GetChildren() then
				if table.find(arg, v7.Name) and v7:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and v7:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and v7.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
					tp({ Target = v7.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 200, 0) })
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

obj.Entity.find = function(arg)
	local children, children2

	if Sea2 then
		children = game:GetService("Workspace").SeaMonster:GetChildren()
		-- [Kingahah] GhostMonster only exists while a Ghost Ship event is running, so
		-- this threw in Sea 2 at every other time and killed every Sea2 farm loop
		local ghostFolder = game.Workspace:FindFirstChild("GhostMonster")
		children2 = ghostFolder and ghostFolder:GetChildren() or {}
	elseif Sea3 then
		children = game:GetService("Workspace").SeaMonster:GetChildren()
		children2 = Repli.MOB:GetChildren()
	else
		children = Repli.MOB:GetChildren()
		children2 = Repli.MOB:GetChildren()
	end

	local function fn9(arg2)
		return arg2:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and arg2.Humanoid.Health > 0
	end

	local function fn10(arg2)
		for _, v4 in pairs(arg2) do
			if table.find(arg, v4.Name) and fn9(v4) then
				return true
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	return fn10(workspace.Monster.Mon:GetChildren()) or fn10(workspace.Monster.Boss:GetChildren()) or fn10(children2) or fn10(children) or fn10(Repli.MOB:GetChildren())
end

checkloop = {}

obj.getPcall = function(arg, arg2)
	checkloop[arg] = checkloop[arg] or {}
	checkloop[arg].Value = checkloop[arg].Value or 0
	local ok, result = pcall(arg2)

	if not ok and _G.Debug then
		local v4 = debug.getinfo(3, "Sl")
		local currentline = v4.currentline or -1
		local name = v4.name or "Unknown Function"
		local v5 = os.date("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
		warn("\n[DEBUG - ERROR]")
		warn("-----------------------------")
		warn("Time:", v5)
		warn("Function Name :", arg .. " ( " .. name .. " )" .. " : Function Line : " .. currentline)
		warn("Error Message:", result)
		warn("-----------------------------\n")
		return
	end
end

local tbl6 = {}
local flag2 = false
-- [Kingahah] this ran the whole quest-table build at script load but the result
-- was never read (the only reader, "Start Select Monster Farm", was indexing it
-- as if it were a single quest record). Dropping it also stops a possible load
-- time abort: GetQuestData now returns nil on an empty quest table.
local v4 = obj.GetQuestData()

obj["Auto Farm Level"] = function()
	flag = Config["Auto Farm Level"]

	while Config["Auto Farm Level"] and task.wait(0.1) do
		obj.getPcall("Auto Farm Level", function()
			if InRaid then
				return
			end

			if not flag then
				return
			end

			if Client.PlayerStats.lvl.Value >= 4900 and not flag2 then
				flag2 = true
				Windown.Notify("error", { text = "If you are Lv 4.9k or higher,just Clear new Dungeon but it so hart farm", time = 10 })
			end

			local v5

			if Config["Not Accept Boss"] then
				v5 = obj.GetQuestData(nil, true)
			else
				v5 = obj.GetQuestData()
			end

			local cFrame = v5.NPC.CFrame

			if Client.CurrentQuest.Value ~= v5.QuestTitle or Client.CurrentQuest.Value == "" then
				tp({ Target = cFrame })
				Repli:WaitForChild("Chest").Remotes.Functions.Quest:InvokeServer("take", v5.QuestTitle)
			elseif Client.CurrentQuest.Value == v5.QuestTitle then
				if v5.Mob == "Dough Master [Lv. 3275]" and v5.LevelRequired == 3275 then
					tp({ Target = CFrame.new(30279.0625, 69.364418029785156, 93166.2734375) })
				else
					local v6 = Repli.MOB:FindFirstChild(v5.Mob)

					if monsterPot[v5.Mob] then
						tp({ Target = monsterPot[v5.Mob] * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0) })
					elseif v6 then
						tp({ Target = v6:GetModelCFrame() * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0) })
					elseif v5.LevelRequired >= 3250 and not tbl6[v5.Mob] then
						fn7(v5, cFrame)
					else
						tp({ Target = tbl6[v5.Mob] or cFrame })
					end
				end

				if obj.Entity.find({ v5.Mob }) then
					obj.Entity.attack({ v5.Mob }, "Auto Farm Level")

					if not tbl6[v5.Mob] then
						tbl6[v5.Mob] = Client.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

obj["Auto Second Sea"] = function()
	local str5 = "Auto Second Sea"

	while Config[str5] and task.wait(0.1) do
		obj.getPcall("Auto Second Sea", function()
			if InRaid then
				return
			end

			if Sea1 and Client.PlayerStats.lvl.Value >= 2250 and Client.PlayerStats.lvl.Value < 4000 then
				if flag then
					while true do
						wait()
						flag = false
						if not (not Config[str5] or not flag) then
							continue
						end
						break
					end
				end

				if Client.PlayerStats.SecondSeaProgression.Value == "Yes" then
					getQuestOld(FF["Teleport To Sea 2"], 2)
				elseif obj.getPlayerMaterial("Map") > 0 then
					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC.Traveler.CFrame)
					wait(0.5)
				elseif not GUI.MainGui.QuestFrame.QuestBoard.Visible then
					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC.Traveler.CFrame)
					wait(0.5)
				else
					if Repli.MOB:FindFirstChild("Seasoned Fishman [Lv. 2200]") then
						tp({ Target = Repli.MOB:FindFirstChild("Seasoned Fishman [Lv. 2200]"):GetPivot() })
					else
						tp({
							Target = CFrame.new(-1865.43481, 45.2696266, 6722.8501, 0.965929627, 0, -0.258804798, 0, 1, 0, 0.258804798, 0, 0.965929627),
						})
					end

					if obj.Entity.find({ "Seasoned Fishman [Lv. 2200]" }) then
						obj.Entity.attack({ "Seasoned Fishman [Lv. 2200]" }, "Auto Second Sea")
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

local tbl7 = {}

HopServer = function()
	local str5 = "auto_hop"
	if not (Config and Config[str5]) then
		return
	end
	local HttpService_ = game:GetService("HttpService")
	local TeleportService_ = game:GetService("TeleportService")
	local str6 = "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/"
	local placeId = game.PlaceId
	local id = nil

	local function fn9(arg)
		if not Config[str5] then
			return
		end
		local str7 = str6 .. placeId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"

		if arg and arg ~= "" then
			str7 ..= "&cursor=" .. arg
		end

		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			local body = safeHttpGet(str7)

			if not body then
				return nil
			end

			return HttpService_:JSONDecode(body)
		end)

		if ok and result then
			return result
		end
	end

	local str7 = ""
	local exitTo = nil

	while true do
		if not Config[str5] then
			exitTo = 1
			break
		else
			local v5 = fn9(str7)

			if not v5 then
				exitTo = 2
				break
			else
				local exitTo2 = nil
				local picked = nil

				for _, v6 in pairs(v5.data) do
					if not Config[str5] then
						exitTo2 = 1
						break
					elseif v6.id ~= game.JobId and v6.playing < v6.maxPlayers and not tbl7[v6.id] then
						exitTo2 = 2
						picked = v6
						break
					end
				end

				if exitTo2 ~= 1 then
					if exitTo2 == 2 then
						-- [Kingahah] was `s4.id`; `s4` is an undefined global left
						-- over from deobfuscation, so Smart Hop threw
						-- "index a nil value" the moment it found a server and
						-- never teleported
						tbl7[picked.id] = true
						id = picked.id
					end

					str7 = v5.nextPageCursor

					if id or not str7 then
						exitTo = 2
						break
					else
						continue
					end
				end
			end

			break
		end
	end

	if exitTo == 1 then
		return
	end

	if exitTo == 2 then
		if id and Config[str5] then
			print("Smart Hop → Joining new server:", id)
			local queueOnTeleport2 = queue_on_teleport or syn and syn.queue_on_teleport or fluxus and fluxus.queue_on_teleport

			if queueOnTeleport2 then
				local queued2, err2 = pcall(queueOnTeleport2, "loadstring(game:HttpGet('" .. str .. "'))()")

				if queued2 then
					print("Queued auto-exec")
				else
					warn("[fixed] Failed to queue auto-exec: " .. tostring(err2))
				end
			else
				warn("queue_on_teleport not supported")
			end

			pcall(function()
				TeleportService_:TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId, id, game.Players.LocalPlayer)
			end)
		else
			warn("No available server found.")
		end

		return
	end
end

local tbl8 = {
	FuryTentacle = "Chaos Kraken",
	["ThirdSeaEldritch Crab"] = "Deepsea Crusher",
	ThirdSeaDragon = "Drakenfyr the Inferno King",
	SeaDragon = "Abyssal Tyrant",
	["Skull King"] = "Skull King",
	["Ghost Ship"] = "Ghost Ship",
	HydraSeaKing = "Hydra Sea King",
	SeaKing = "Sea King",
}

local function fn9(arg)
	return tbl8[arg] or arg
end

obj.auto_hop = function()
	local str5 = "auto_hop"

	while Config and Config[str5] and task.wait(0.5) do
		obj.getPcall("auto_hop", function()
			local selectedSeaMonsters = Config.SelectedSeaMonsters or {}
			if #selectedSeaMonsters == 0 then
				warn("No entities selected for auto hop.")
				return
			end
			local flag3 = false

			for _, selectedSeaMonster in pairs(selectedSeaMonsters) do
				if obj.Entity.find({ selectedSeaMonster }) then
					print("Entity found:", fn9(selectedSeaMonster), "→ stopping hop")
					Config[str5] = false
					flag3 = true
					break
				end
			end

			if not flag3 then
				local hopDelay = Config.HopDelay or 15
				print("No entity found → hopping in", hopDelay, "seconds")
				task.wait(hopDelay)

				if Config[str5] then
					HopServer()
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

obj.Auto_Farm_Nearest_Mob = function()
	while Config and Config.Auto_Farm_Nearest_Mob and task.wait(0.2) do
		obj.getPcall("Auto_Farm_Nearest_Mob", function()
			if InRaid then
				return
			end
			local character = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character
			if not character or not character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				return
			end
			local position = character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
			local huge = math.huge
			local v5 = nil

			for _, child in pairs(workspace.Monster.Mon:GetChildren()) do
				local humanoid = child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
				local humanoidRootPart = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if humanoid and humanoidRootPart and humanoid.Health > 0 then
					local magnitude = (position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude

					if magnitude < huge then
						huge = magnitude
						v5 = child
					end
				end
			end

			if v5 and huge <= 1000 then
				if obj.Entity.find({ v5.Name }) then
					obj.Entity.attack({ v5.Name }, "Auto_Farm_Nearest_Mob")
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

local n = 0
local flag3 = false

seaChest = function(arg)
	local function fn10()
		if Config["Auto Sea King"] or arg then
			for _, v5 in ipairs({ "Legacy Island1", "Legacy Island2", "Legacy Island3", "Legacy Island4" }) do
				local v6 = game:GetService("Workspace").Island:FindFirstChild(v5)
				if v6 then
					return v6
				end
			end
		end

		return nil
	end

	local function fn11()
		if Config["Auto Hydra Sea King"] or arg then
			local v5 = pairs
			local island = workspace:FindFirstChild("Island")

			for _, child in v5(island:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name:match("Sea King") then
					return true
				end
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	local function fn12()
		if Config["Auto Ghost Ship"] then
			for _, child in pairs(game.Workspace:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name:match("Chest") and not child:FindFirstChild("Zen Hub") then
					warn("[DEBUG] : Find Chest")
					return true
				end
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	local function fn13(arg2)
		for _, child in pairs(arg2:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name:match("Chest") then
				warn("[DEBUG] : Find Chest")
				return true
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	local function fn14()
		if Config.Monter_Hop or Config["Auto Third World"] or arg then
			if flag3 then
				task.wait(3.5)
				HopServer(true)

				delay(5, function()
					HopServer()
				end)

				return
			end

			-- [Kingahah] GetAttribute returns nil until the server replicates the
			-- timer, and indexing nil threw before the hop decision could run
			local spawnText = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):GetAttribute("SeaMonsterSpawnText") or ""
			local match, v5, v6 = spawnText:match("(%d+):(%d+):(%d+)")
			local n2 = (tonumber(match) or 0) * 60 + (tonumber(v5) or 0) + (tonumber(v6) or 0) / 60

			if _G.Debug then
				warn("[Debug] :\n", Config["Setting Sea Monster Time (for Hop)"], "\ntotalMinutes =", n2, "\nHOP =", arg, "\n(checkNeedHop Sea 2)")
			end

			if Config["Setting Sea Monster Time (for Hop)"] < n2 then
				task.wait(3.5)
				HopServer(true)

				delay(5, function()
					HopServer()
				end)
			end
		end
	end

	if not game:GetService("Workspace").SeaMonster:FindFirstChild("HydraSeaKing") and fn11() then
		print("HydraSeaKing")
		local v5 = pairs
		local island = workspace:FindFirstChild("Island")

		for _, child in v5(island:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name:match("Sea King") then
				if fn13(child) then
					tp({ Target = child.HydraStand.CFrame })
					Client.PlayerStats.beli.Value = Client.PlayerStats.beli.Value + 50
					wait(3)
					local position = Client.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position

					if dist(child:FindFirstChild("SeaBeastChest").ChestTop.Position, position) < 10 and Client.PlayerStats.beli.Value > n + 1 then
						print(0, " : ", Client.PlayerStats.beli.Value)
						flag3 = true
						fn14()
					end
				else
					tp({ Target = child.HydraStand.CFrame })
				end
			end
		end
	elseif not workspace.SeaMonster:FindFirstChild("SeaKing") and fn10() then
		for _, v5 in ipairs({ "Legacy Island1", "Legacy Island2", "Legacy Island3", "Legacy Island4" }) do
			local v6 = game:GetService("Workspace").Island:FindFirstChild(v5)

			if v6 and fn13(v6.ChestSpawner) then
				tp({ Target = v6.ChestSpawner.CFrame })
				Client.PlayerStats.beli.Value = Client.PlayerStats.beli.Value + 50
				wait(3)

				if dist(v6.ChestSpawner.Position, Client.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position) < 10 and Client.PlayerStats.beli.Value > n + 1 then
					print(0, " : ", Client.PlayerStats.beli.Value)
					flag3 = true
					fn14()
				end
			elseif v6 then
				tp({ Target = v6.ChestSpawner.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 50, 0) })
			end
		end
	elseif not workspace.SeaMonster:FindFirstChild("Ghost Ship") and fn12() then
		print("Ghost Ship")

		for _, child in pairs(game.Workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name:match("Chest") and not child:FindFirstChild("Zen Hub") then
				tp({ Target = child.PrimaryPart.CFrame })

				if dist(child.PrimaryPart.Position, Client.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position) < 10 then
					if not child:FindFirstChild("Zen Hub") then
						Instance.new("Folder", child).Name = "Zen Hub"
					end

					wait(1.5)
				end
			end
		end
	else
		print("Run Hop")
		fn14()
	end
end

obj["Auto Third World"] = function()
	local str5 = "Auto Third World"

	while Config[str5] and task.wait(0.1) do
		obj.getPcall("Auto Third World", function()
			if InRaid then
				return
			end

			if Client.PlayerStats.lvl.Value >= 4000 and not Sea3 then
				if flag then
					while true do
						wait()
						flag = false
						if not (not Config[str5] or not flag) then
							continue
						end
						break
					end
				end

				if obj.getPlayerMaterial("Kraken's Cache") > 0 then
					for i = 1, 15 do
						wait(0.3)
						getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC:FindFirstChild("The Squid").CFrame)
					end
				elseif obj.Entity.find({ "Tentacle" }) then
					obj.Entity.attack({ "Tentacle" }, "Auto Third World")
				elseif obj.getPlayerMaterial("Heart of Sea") > 0 then
					if Client.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("CraftingMaterialUI") then
						Client.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("CraftingMaterialUI"):Destroy()
						Client.Character.Humanoid:ChangeState(15)
					else
						getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC:FindFirstChild("Summon Tentacle").CFrame)
					end
				else
					if obj.getPlayerMaterial("Kraken's Cache") > 0 then
						return
					end

					if not obj.Entity.find({ "Tentacle" }) then
						local tbl9 = {
							Log = 50,
							["Pile of Bones"] = 10,
							["Fresh Fish"] = 50,
							["Angellic's Feather"] = 14,
							["Undead's Ooze"] = 10,
							["Sea King's Blood"] = 1,
						}

						local tbl10 = {
							Sea1 = workspace.AllNPC:FindFirstChild("Elite Pirate").CFrame,
							Sea2 = workspace.AllNPC:FindFirstChild("Elite Pirate").CFrame,
						}

						local log = tbl9.Log

						if obj.getPlayerMaterial("Log") < log then
							if not Sea2 then
								getQuestOld(tbl10.Sea2)
								return
							end
							local v5 = pairs
							local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

							for _, descendant in v5(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
								if string.find(descendant.Name, "Tree") and descendant:FindFirstChild("Part") and descendant.Part.Transparency == 0 then
									task.wait(1.5)
									local flag4 = Config[str5]

									if not flag4 then
										local log2 = tbl9.Log
										flag4 = obj.getPlayerMaterial("Log") <= log2
									end

									if flag4 or not obj.Entity.find({ "Tentacle" }) then
										while true do
											wait()
											tp({ Target = descendant:GetModelCFrame() })

											if not Client.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Bisento") and not Client.Character:FindFirstChild("Bisento") then
												game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Chest"):WaitForChild("Remotes"):WaitForChild("Functions"):WaitForChild("InventoryEq"):InvokeServer(unpack({ "Bisento" }))
											end

											EquipTools("Bisento")

											if not tbl.CheckOnCooldown("Z") or not tbl.CheckOnCooldown("X") then
												if not tbl.CheckSkillLock("Z") then
													game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(true, "Z", false, game)
													game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(false, "Z", false, game)
												end

												if not tbl.CheckSkillLock("X") then
													game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(true, "X", false, game)
													game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(false, "X", false, game)
												end
											end

											if not (not Config[str5] or tbl.CheckOnCooldown("Z") or tbl.CheckOnCooldown("X") or obj.Entity.find({ "Tentacle" }) or obj.getPlayerMaterial("Kraken's Cache") > 0) then
												continue
											end
											break
										end
									end
								end

								local flag4 = not Config[str5]

								if not flag4 then
									local log2 = tbl9.Log
									flag4 = obj.getPlayerMaterial("Log") >= log2
								end

								if not (flag4 or obj.Entity.find({ "Tentacle" }) or obj.getPlayerMaterial("Kraken's Cache") > 0) then
									continue
								end
								break
							end
						else
							local pileOfBones = tbl9["Pile of Bones"]

							if obj.getPlayerMaterial("Pile of Bones") < pileOfBones then
								if not Sea2 then
									getQuestOld(tbl10.Sea2)
								elseif obj.Entity.find({ "Skull Pirate [Lv. 3050]" }) then
									obj.Entity.attack({ "Skull Pirate [Lv. 3050]" }, "Auto Third World")
								else
									tp({
										Target = CFrame.new(-5996.76953125, 462.46005249023438, 7296.43115234375) * CFrame.new(0, -50, 0),
									})
								end
							else
								local freshFish = tbl9["Fresh Fish"]

								if obj.getPlayerMaterial("Fresh Fish") < freshFish then
									if not Sea1 then
										getQuestOld(tbl10.Sea1)
									elseif obj.Entity.find({
											"Karate Fishman [Lv. 200]",
											"Fighter Fishman [Lv. 180]",
											"Shark Man [Lv. 230]",
										}) then
										obj.Entity.attack({
											"Karate Fishman [Lv. 200]",
											"Fighter Fishman [Lv. 180]",
											"Shark Man [Lv. 230]",
										}, "Auto Third World")
									else
										tp({
											Target = workspace.Island["D - Shark Island"].D.Base:GetChildren()[57].CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 50, 0),
										})
									end
								else
									local angellicSFeather = tbl9["Angellic's Feather"]

									if obj.getPlayerMaterial("Angellic's Feather") < angellicSFeather then
										if not Sea1 then
											getQuestOld(tbl10.Sea1)
										elseif obj.Entity.find({ "Sky Soldier [Lv. 800]", "Ball Man [Lv. 850]" }) then
											obj.Entity.attack({ "Sky Soldier [Lv. 800]", "Ball Man [Lv. 850]" }, "Auto Third World")
										else
											tp({
												Target = workspace.Island["H - Skyland"].Sky.Base:GetChildren()[4].CFrame,
											})
										end
									else
										local undeadSOoze = tbl9["Undead's Ooze"]

										if obj.getPlayerMaterial("Undead's Ooze") < undeadSOoze then
											if not Sea1 then
												getQuestOld(tbl10.Sea1)
											elseif obj.Entity.find({ "Zombie [Lv. 1500]" }) then
												obj.Entity.attack({ "Zombie [Lv. 1500]" }, "Auto Third World")
											elseif monsterPot["Zombie [Lv. 1500]"] then
												tp({
													Target = monsterPot["Zombie [Lv. 1500]"] * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0),
												})
											end
										else
											local seaKingSBlood = tbl9["Sea King's Blood"]

											if obj.getPlayerMaterial("Sea King's Blood") < seaKingSBlood then
												if not Sea2 then
													getQuestOld(tbl10.Sea2)
												elseif obj.Entity.find({ "SeaKing", "HydraSeaKing" }) then
													obj.Entity.attack({ "SeaKing", "HydraSeaKing" }, "Auto Third World")
												else
													seaChest(true)
												end
											elseif not Sea2 then
												getQuestOld(tbl10.Sea2)
											else
												getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC:FindFirstChild("Jack Stones").CFrame)

												for _, child in pairs(Client.PlayerGui:GetChildren()) do
													if child.Name == "CraftingMaterialUI" then
														if child:FindFirstChild("Frame") and child.Frame.OrbName.Text ~= "Heart of Sea" then
															child.Frame.Materials.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
															child.Frame.Materials.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
															child.Frame.Materials.Visible = true

															if child.Frame.Materials:FindFirstChild("ScrollingFrame") then
																child.Frame.Materials:FindFirstChild("ScrollingFrame").ClipsDescendants = false

																if child.Frame.Materials:FindFirstChild("ScrollingFrame"):FindFirstChild("UIGridLayout") then
																	child.Frame.Materials:FindFirstChild("ScrollingFrame"):FindFirstChild("UIGridLayout").CellSize = UDim2.new(1001, 0, 1001, 0)
																end

																if child.Frame.Materials:FindFirstChild("ScrollingFrame"):FindFirstChild("Diamond Key") then
																	child.Frame.Materials:FindFirstChild("ScrollingFrame"):FindFirstChild("Diamond Key").Visible = false
																end

																if child.Frame:FindFirstChild("CraftButton") then
																	child.Frame.CraftButton.Size = UDim2.new(1001, 0, 1001, 0)
																end

																game:GetService("VirtualUser"):Button1Down(Vector2.new(1, 1))
																game:GetService("VirtualUser"):Button1Up(Vector2.new(1, 1))

																task.delay(3, function()
																	if child and child.Parent then
																		child:Destroy()
																	end
																end)
															end
														elseif child.Frame.OrbName.Text == "Heart of Sea" then
															child.Frame.CraftButton.Size = UDim2.new(1001, 0, 1001, 0)
															game:GetService("VirtualUser"):Button1Down(Vector2.new(1, 1))
															game:GetService("VirtualUser"):Button1Up(Vector2.new(1, 1))
														end
													end
												end
											end
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

obj.getPlayerFruitStore = function(arg)

			return { Num = v5, Value = true }

local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new()

tweenModel = function(arg, arg2)
	local cFrameValue = Instance.new("CFrameValue")
	cFrameValue.Value = arg:GetPrimaryPartCFrame()

	cFrameValue:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(function()
		arg:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(cFrameValue.Value)
	end)

	local tween = TweenService:Create(cFrameValue, tweenInfo, { Value = arg2 })
	tween:Play()

	tween.Completed:Connect(function()
		cFrameValue:Destroy()
	end)
end

checkTitle = function(arg)
	for k in pairs(game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(Client.PlayerStats.TitleStore.Value)) do
		if k == arg then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

local function fn12(...)
	if _G.Debug then
		local tbl9 = { ... }

		if #tbl9 > 0 then
			warn("[Debug]", unpack(tbl9))
		end
	end
end

_G.D = nil

spin = function(arg)
	-- [Kingahah] this was never cleaned up: every call stacked another
	-- BodyThrust and another Stepped connection that permanently disabled
	-- collision on the torso parts, so the character kept getting shoved.
	local character = Client and Client.Character
	local rootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

	if not rootPart then
		return
	end

	if _G.D then
		_G.D:Disconnect()
		_G.D = nil
	end

	if _G.SpinThrust then
		_G.SpinThrust:Destroy()
		_G.SpinThrust = nil
	end

	restoreCollision()

	_G.D = game:GetService("RunService").Stepped:Connect(function()
		local char = Client and Client.Character

		if not char then
			return
		end

		for _, name in ipairs({ "Head", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "HumanoidRootPart", "Torso" }) do
			local part = char:FindFirstChild(name)

			if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
				part.CanCollide = false
			end
		end
	end)

	local bodyThrust = Instance.new("BodyThrust")
	bodyThrust.Name = "KingahahSpin"
	bodyThrust.Parent = rootPart
	bodyThrust.Force = Vector3.new(arg, 0, arg)
	bodyThrust.Location = rootPart.Position
	_G.SpinThrust = bodyThrust
end

stopSpin = function()
	if _G.D then
		_G.D:Disconnect()
		_G.D = nil
	end

	if _G.SpinThrust then
		_G.SpinThrust:Destroy()
		_G.SpinThrust = nil
	end

	local char = Client and Client.Character

	if char then
		for _, name in ipairs({ "Head", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "HumanoidRootPart", "Torso" }) do
			local part = char:FindFirstChild(name)

			if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
				pcall(function()
					part.CanCollide = true
				end)
			end
		end
	end

	restoreCollision()
end

local function fn13(arg, arg2, arg3)
	local flag4 = arg3 or false

	if arg.Text == "Peaceful Sea" then
		delay(5, function()
			if arg.Text == "Peaceful Sea" then
				HopServer(true)

				delay(15, function()
					HopServer()
				end)
			end
		end)

		return
	end

	if arg.Text == "Set sail!" then
		fn12("Now Sea =", "Set sail!")
		tp({ Target = CFrame.new(-5730.40625, 0.68508493900299072, 6110.42431640625) })
		return
	end

	local match, v5, v6 = arg.Text:match("(%d+):(%d+):(%d+)")
	-- [Kingahah] unguarded tonumber(nil) * 60 throws whenever the label is not
	-- exactly "H:MM:SS"; the guarded twin at 2489 shows the intended shape
	local n2 = (tonumber(match) or 0) * 60 + (tonumber(v5) or 0) + (tonumber(v6) or 0) / 60

	if _G.Debug then
		warn("[Debug] :\n", Config["Setting Sea Monster Time (for Hop)"], "\ntotalMinutes =", n2, "\nHOP =", flag4, "\n(checkNeedHop)")
	end

	if n2 > arg2 and flag4 then
		HopServer(true)

		delay(15, function()
			HopServer()
		end)
	end
end

obj["Auto Kioru 2"] = function()
	local str5 = "Auto Kioru 2"

	while Config[str5] and task.wait(0.1) do
		obj.getPcall("Auto Kioru 2", function()
			if InRaid then
				return
			end

			if Client.Inventory:FindFirstChild("Kioru V2") then
				return
			end

			if _G.Debug then
				warn("[DEBUG]", "checkTitle =", checkTitle("Tyrant Slayer"), "Tyrant Slayer")
				warn("[DEBUG]", "checkTitle =", checkTitle("Krakenbane"), "Krakenbane")
				warn("[DEBUG]", "checkTitle =", checkTitle("Shellbreaker"), "Shellbreaker")
				warn("[DEBUG]", "checkTitle =", checkTitle("Dragonbane"), "Dragonbane")
			end

			if checkTitle("Tyrant Slayer") and checkTitle("Krakenbane") and checkTitle("Shellbreaker") and checkTitle("Dragonbane") then
				while true do
					wait()
					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Kioru V2 Quest"].CFrame, "Button1")
					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Kioru V2 Quest"].CFrame)
					if not (not Config[str5] or Client.Inventory:FindFirstChild("Kioru V2")) then
						continue
					end
					break
				end

				while true do
					wait()
					flag = true
					if not (not Config[str5] or flag) then
						continue
					end
					break
				end
			elseif obj.Entity.find({ "ThirdSeaDragon", "ThirdSeaEldritch Crab", "SeaDragon", "FuryTentacle" }) then
				if flag then
					while true do
						wait()
						flag = false
						if not (not Config[str5] or not flag) then
							continue
						end
						break
					end
				end

				obj.Entity.attack({ "ThirdSeaDragon", "ThirdSeaEldritch Crab", "SeaDragon", "FuryTentacle" }, "Auto Kioru 2")
			else
				fn13(GUI.MainGui.StarterFrame.LegacyPoseFrame.ThirdSea.TextLabel, Config["Setting Sea Monster Time (for Hop)"], true)
			end
		end)
	end
end

local function fn14()
	local tbl9 = {
		["Third Sea Dragon"] = function()
			if Config["Third Sea Dragon Hop"] then
				if not obj.Entity.find({ "ThirdSeaDragon" }) then
					return true
				end
			end

			return false
		end,
		["Deepsea Crusher"] = function()
			if Config["Deepsea Crusher Hop"] then
				if not obj.Entity.find({ "ThirdSeaEldritch Crab" }) then
					return true
				end
			end

			return false
		end,
		["Sea Dragon"] = function()
			if Config["Sea Dragon Hop"] then
				if not obj.Entity.find({ "SeaDragon" }) then
					return true
				end
			end

			return false
		end,
		["Chaos Kraken"] = function()
			if Config["Chaos Kraken Hop"] then
				if not obj.Entity.find({ "FuryTentacle" }) then
					return true
				end
			end

			return false
		end,
		["Shark Galleon Boss"] = function()
			if Config["Shark Galleon Boss Hop"] then
				if not obj.Entity.find({ "Shark Galleon Boss" }) then
					return true
				end
			end

			return false
		end,
		Serpent = function()
			if Config["Serpent Hop"] then
				if not obj.Entity.find({ "Serpent" }) then
					return true
				end
			end

			return false
		end,
	}

	if tbl9["Third Sea Dragon"]() and tbl9["Deepsea Crusher"]() and tbl9["Sea Dragon"]() and tbl9["Chaos Kraken"]() and tbl9["Shark Galleon Boss"]() and tbl9.Serpent() then
		fn12("Not Have Sea Monster Wait For Hop")
		fn13(GUI.MainGui.StarterFrame.LegacyPoseFrame.ThirdSea.TextLabel, Config["Setting Sea Monster Time (for Hop)"], true)
	elseif Config["Chaos Kraken Hop"] or Config["Deepsea Crusher Hop"] or Config["Sea Dragon Hop"] or Config["Third Sea Dragon Hop"] then
		fn13(GUI.MainGui.StarterFrame.LegacyPoseFrame.ThirdSea.TextLabel, Config["Setting Sea Monster Time (for Hop)"], true)
	end
end

obj.hopserver = function()
	local HttpService_ = game:GetService("HttpService")
	local TeleportService_ = game:GetService("TeleportService")
	local Players_ = game:GetService("Players")
	local placeId = game.PlaceId
	local jobId = game.JobId
	local str5 = "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. placeId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"

	local function fn15(arg)
		return safeServerList(str5 .. (arg and "&cursor=" .. arg or ""))
	end

	local nextPageCursor = nil
	local huge = math.huge
	local v5 = nil

	while true do
		local v6 = fn15(nextPageCursor)

		for _, v7 in pairs(v6.data) do
			if v7.id ~= jobId and v7.playing < v7.maxPlayers then
				if v7.playing < huge then
					huge = v7.playing
					v5 = v7
				end
			end
		end

		nextPageCursor = v6.nextPageCursor
		if not (not nextPageCursor or huge <= 1) then
			continue
		end
		break
	end

	if v5 then
		TeleportService_:TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId, v5.id, Players_.LocalPlayer)
	end
end

HopServer1 = function()
	local HttpService_ = game:GetService("HttpService")
	local TeleportService_ = game:GetService("TeleportService")
	local Players_ = game:GetService("Players")
	local placeId = game.PlaceId
	local jobId = game.JobId
	local str5 = "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. placeId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"

	local function fn15(arg)
		return safeServerList(str5 .. (arg and "&cursor=" .. arg or ""))
	end

	local nextPageCursor = nil
	local huge = math.huge
	local v5 = nil

	while true do
		local v6 = fn15(nextPageCursor)

		for _, v7 in pairs(v6.data) do
			if v7.id ~= jobId and v7.playing < v7.maxPlayers then
				if v7.playing < huge then
					huge = v7.playing
					v5 = v7
				end
			end
		end

		nextPageCursor = v6.nextPageCursor
		if not (not nextPageCursor or huge <= 1) then
			continue
		end
		break
	end

	if v5 then
		TeleportService_:TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId, v5.id, Players_.LocalPlayer)
	end
end

game:GetService("TeleportService")
game:GetService("Players")
local flag4 = false

SafeHop = function()
	if flag4 then
		return
	end
	flag4 = true

	for i = 1, 5 do
		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			HopServer1()
		end)

		if not ok then
			warn("Hop failed, retrying...", i, result)
			task.wait(2)
			continue
		end

		break
	end

	flag4 = false
end
