local tbl9 = {}
local v5 = nil

GetCurrentWeapon = function()
	for _, child in pairs(Client.Character:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("Tool") then
			return child.Name
		end
	end

	return nil
end

obj["Auto Serpent"] = function()
	while Config["Auto Serpent"] and task.wait() do
		obj.getPcall("Auto Serpent", function()
			if InRaid then
				return
			end
			local basicRod = Client.Character:FindFirstChild("Basic Rod")
			local humanoidRootPart = Client.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if obj.Entity.find({ "Serpent" }) then
				if basicRod and v5 then
					game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Chest.Remotes.Functions.InventoryEq:InvokeServer(v5)
				end

				obj.Entity.attack({ "Serpent" }, "Auto Serpent")
			elseif workspace.Effects:FindFirstChild("SerpentWhirlpool") then
				local pivot = workspace.Effects.SerpentWhirlpool:GetPivot()

				if not Client.Character:FindFirstChild("Basic Rod") then
					v5 = GetCurrentWeapon()
					game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Chest.Remotes.Functions.InventoryEq:InvokeServer("Basic Rod")
				end

				if Client.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("FishingUI") then
					for _, v6 in next, tbl9, nil do
						task.cancel(v6)
					end

					-- [Kingahah] tbl9 only ever grew, so this loop walked every dead
					-- thread handle ever created, ~60x/second, and never freed them
					table.clear(tbl9)

					Client.PlayerGui.FishingUI.FishingBackground.FishingBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1.4, 0)
				else
					tbl9[#tbl9 + 1] = task.spawn(function()
						local tbl10 = { "SW_Basic Rod_M1", { Charge = math.huge, MouseHit = pivot } }
						game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Chest.Remotes.Functions.SkillAction:InvokeServer(unpack(tbl10))
					end)
				end

				EquipTools("Basic Rod")

				if humanoidRootPart then
					humanoidRootPart.CFrame = pivot + Vector3.new(0, 12, 0)
				end
			else
				fn14()
			end
		end)
	end
end

local tbl10 = {}

obj["Auto Kill Minion"] = function()
	local function fn15(arg, arg2)
		local tbl11 = {}

		for _, descendant in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("Model") and descendant:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and descendant:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
				if string.find(descendant.Name, "Minion") or string.find(descendant.Name, "Boss") then
					if (descendant.HumanoidRootPart.Position - arg).Magnitude <= arg2 then
						table.insert(tbl11, descendant.Name)
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return tbl11
	end

	while Config["Auto Kill Minion"] and task.wait() do
		obj.getPcall("Auto Kill Minion", function()
			if InRaid then
				return
			end
			local flag5 = false

			for _, child in pairs(workspace.EventSpawns:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name == "Spawn" then
					Client.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = child.CFrame
					task.wait(0.5)
					local v6 = fn15(child.Position, 200)

					if #v6 > 0 then
						obj.Entity.attack(v6, "Auto Kill Minion")
						flag5 = true
						break
					elseif child:FindFirstChild("Chest") then
						local cFrame = child.Chest.RootPart.CFrame

						if not tbl10[cFrame] then
							tbl10[cFrame] = true
							tp({ Target = cFrame })
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if not flag5 then
				task.wait(1)
			end
		end)
	end
end

local tbl11 = {}

obj["Auto Kill Minion1"] = function()
	-- [Kingahah] Config["Minion Tier"] / Config["Boss Tier"] were read here but never
-- declared (no default, no slider), so the comparison was always against nil and
-- this function always returned an empty table -> "Auto Kill Minion1" never
-- attacked anything.
	local minionTier = Config["Minion Tier"] or 1
	local bossTier = Config["Boss Tier"] or 1

	local function fn15()
		local tbl12 = {}
		local tbl13 = obj.Entity.find({ "Bandit" }, true)
		local v6 = pairs
		tbl13 = tbl13 or {}

		for _, v7 in v6(tbl13) do
			local name = v7.Name or ""

			if string.find(name, "Bandit Minion") or string.find(name, "Bandit Boss") then
				local num = tonumber(string.match(name, "Tier%s*(%d+)"))

				if num then
					if string.find(name, "Bandit Minion") and num == minionTier then
						table.insert(tbl12, name)
					elseif string.find(name, "Bandit Boss") and num == bossTier then
						table.insert(tbl12, name)
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return tbl12
	end

	while Config["Auto Kill Minion1"] and task.wait() do
		obj.getPcall("Auto Kill Minion1", function()
			if InRaid then
				return
			end
			local children = workspace.EventSpawns:GetChildren()
			local flag5 = false

			for _, child in pairs(children) do
				if child.Name == "Spawn" and child:FindFirstChild("Chest") then
					local cFrame = child.Chest.RootPart.CFrame

					if not tbl11[cFrame] then
						tbl11[cFrame] = true
						tp({ Target = cFrame })

						for i = 1, 7 do
							local v6 = workspace:FindFirstChild("Chest" .. i)

							if v6 and v6:FindFirstChild("RootPart") then
								Client.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = v6.RootPart.CFrame
							end
						end
					end
				end

				local v6 = fn15()

				if #v6 > 0 then
					obj.Entity.attack(v6, "Auto Kill Minion1")
					flag5 = true
					break
				end
			end

			if not flag5 then
				for _, child in pairs(children) do
					if child.Name == "Spawn" then
						Client.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = child.CFrame
						task.wait(1)
						local v6 = fn15()
						if #v6 > 0 then
							obj.Entity.attack(v6, "Auto Kill Minion1")
							break
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

local function fn15(arg)
	local currentCamera = game.Workspace.CurrentCamera

	if arg and arg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
		local humanoidRootPart = arg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local vector = Vector3.zero

		if not humanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("Velocity") then
			vector = Vector3.zero
		end

		currentCamera.CFrame = currentCamera.CFrame:Lerp(CFrame.new(currentCamera.CFrame.p, humanoidRootPart.Position + vector), 0.2)
	end
end

vv = nil
ShiftLock = false
local n2 = 0

game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()
	local now = os.clock()
	if now - n2 < 0.05 then
		return
	end
	n2 = now

	pcall(function()
		if vv then
			fn15(vv)
		end
	end)
end)

task.spawn(function()
	local VirtualInputManager2 = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
	local holdingI = false

	while task.wait(0.3) do
		pcall(function()
			-- [Kingahah] the key was pressed down here and never released, so once
			-- "Auto Clear Dungeon" had been switched on at any point the I key stayed
			-- logically held for the rest of the session
			if Config["Auto Clear Dungeon"] then
				if not holdingI then
					VirtualInputManager2:SendKeyEvent(true, "I", false, game)
					holdingI = true
				end
			elseif holdingI then
				VirtualInputManager2:SendKeyEvent(false, "I", false, game)
				holdingI = false
			end
		end)
	end
end)

obj["Auto Clear Dungeon"] = function()
	local str5 = "Auto Clear Dungeon"

	while Config[str5] and task.wait(0.1) do
		obj.getPcall("Auto Clear Dungeon", function()
			if not InRaid then
				return
			end

			if Client.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("GoldenArena GUI") and Client.PlayerGui:WaitForChild("GoldenArena GUI"):FindFirstChild("StartButton") then
				-- [Kingahah] this inner `while true` only ever broke on
				-- StartButton.Visible going false, never on the toggle, so if the
				-- button stayed visible the outer loop was wedged forever and the
				-- feature could not be switched back off
				while Config[str5] do
					wait()

					local gui = Client.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("GoldenArena GUI")
					local startButton = gui and gui:FindFirstChild("StartButton")

					if not startButton or not startButton.Visible then
						break
					end

					GuiService.SelectedObject = startButton
					VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, "Return", false, game)
					VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, "Return", false, game)
				end
			end

			local flag5 = not ShiftLock

			if flag5 then
				local windows = Enum.Platform.Windows
				flag5 = game.UserInputService:GetPlatform() ~= windows
			end

			if flag5 then
				ShiftLock = true
				game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(true, "LeftShift", false, game)
				wait(1)
				game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(false, "LeftShift", false, game)
			end

			for _, child in pairs(workspace.MOB:GetChildren()) do
				if child and child.Parent and child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and child.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
					while true do
						if Client.Character.Humanoid.Health / Client.Character.Humanoid.MaxHealth * 100 > 45 then
							game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(false, "E", false, game)

							if Config["Select Type Mode"] == "Magma" then
								tp({
									Target = child.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-15, 170, 5) * CFrame.Angles(-1.5707963267948966, 0, 0),
								})

								local function fn16()
									local cFrame = child.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
									getgenv().PosMonSkill = cFrame
									vv = child

									if not tbl.CheckOnCooldown("Z") and not tbl.CheckSkillLock("Z") then
										game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(true, "Z", false, game)
										game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(false, "Z", false, game)
									end

									if not tbl.CheckOnCooldown("X") and not tbl.CheckSkillLock("X") then
										game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(true, "X", false, game)
										game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(false, "X", false, game)
									end

									if not tbl.CheckOnCooldown("V") and not tbl.CheckSkillLock("V") then
										game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(true, "V", false, game)
										game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(false, "V", false, game)
									end

									if not tbl.CheckOnCooldown("C") and not tbl.CheckSkillLock("C") then
										game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(true, "C", false, game)
										game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(false, "C", false, game)
									end

									if not tbl.CheckOnCooldown("E") and not tbl.CheckSkillLock("E") then
										game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(true, "E", false, game)
										game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(false, "E", false, game)
									end
								end

								EquipTools("MagmaMagma")
								tbl5[#tbl5 + 1] = task.spawn(pcall, fn16)
							else
								local function fn16()
									Attack()
								end

								tbl5[#tbl5 + 1] = task.spawn(pcall, fn16)
								local cFrame = child.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
								getgenv().PosMonSkill = cFrame

								tp({
									Target = child.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 8, 3) * CFrame.Angles(-1.5707963267948966, 0, 0),
								})

								local function fn17()
									useSkill()
								end

								tbl5[#tbl5 + 1] = task.spawn(pcall, fn17)
							end
						else
							while true do
								local n3 = Client.Character.Humanoid.Health / Client.Character.Humanoid.MaxHealth * 100
								tp({ Target = CFrame.new(-5, 21, 89) * CFrame.new(0, 1500, 0) })

								if not Client.Character:FindFirstChild("Cyborg") then
									EquipTools("Cyborg")
								end

								if Client.Character:FindFirstChild("Cyborg") and not tbl.CheckOnCooldown("E") then
									game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(true, "E", false, game)
								end

								if Client.Character:FindFirstChild("Cyborg") and tbl.CheckOnCooldown("E") then
									game:service("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(false, "E", false, game)
								end

								task.wait()
								if not (not Config[str5] or tbl.CheckOnCooldown("E") and n3 > 75) then
									continue
								end
								break
							end
						end

						task.wait()
						if not (not Config[str5] or not child or not child.Parent or not child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or not child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or child.Humanoid.Health <= 0) then
							continue
						end
						break
					end

					if #tbl5 >= 1 then
						for _, v6 in pairs(tbl5) do
							pcall(task.cancel, v6)
						end

						table.clear(tbl5)
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

obj["Auto Kill Jack o lantern"] = function()
	-- [Kingahah] own thread list, was sharing the global one
	local tbl5 = {}
	while Config["Auto Kill Jack o lantern"] and task.wait(0.1) do
		obj.getPcall("Auto Kill Jack o lantern", function()
			if InRaid then
				return
			end

			if obj.Entity.find({ "Jack o lantern [Lv. 10000]" }) then
				obj.Entity.attack({ "Jack o lantern [Lv. 10000]" }, "Auto Kill Jack o lantern")
			elseif not Config["Auto Kill Jack o lantern Fully"] then
				if not Config["Auto Kill Jack o lantern Hop"] then
					if Sea3 then
						tp({ Target = CFrame.new(2828.4, 78.1, 4865.1) })
					elseif Sea2 then
						tp({ Target = CFrame.new(-6070.5, 17, 3787.9) })
					end
				else
					HopServer(true)

					delay(15, function()
						HopServer()
					end)
				end
			elseif Sea3 then
				if obj.getPlayerMaterial("Candy") < 50 then
					if obj.Entity.find({ "Wilderness Gorilla [Lv. 4325]" }) then
						obj.Entity.attack({ "Wilderness Gorilla [Lv. 4325]" }, "Auto Kill Jack o lantern", nil, { Monster = { "Jack o lantern [Lv. 10000]" }, get = "have" })
					else
						tp({ Target = CFrame.new(4861, 48.5, 10181.4) })
					end
				else
					for i = 1, 3 do
						wait(1)
						getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC.SummonJackolantern.CFrame)
					end
				end
			elseif Sea2 then
				if obj.getPlayerMaterial("Candy") < 50 then
					if obj.Entity.find({ "Skull Pirate [Lv. 3050]" }) then
						obj.Entity.attack({ "Skull Pirate [Lv. 3050]" }, "Auto Kill Jack o lantern", nil, { Monster = { "Jack o lantern [Lv. 10000]" }, get = "have" })
					else
						tp({ Target = CFrame.new(-6263.1, 88, 6904.4) })
					end
				else
					for i = 1, 3 do
						wait(1)
						getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC.SummonJackolantern.CFrame)
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

local flag5 = false
tick()

obj["Auto Store Fruit"] = function()
	local str5 = "Auto Store Fruit"

	while Config[str5] and task.wait(0.1) do
		obj.getPcall("Auto Store Fruit", function()
			if InRaid then
				return
			end
			flag5 = true
			local tbl12 = {}

			for k, v6 in pairs(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Chest.Remotes.Functions.GetDFShop:InvokeServer()) do
				if v6 == true then
					table.insert(tbl12, k)
				end
			end

			if table.find(tbl12, Config["Select Fruit Stock"]) and Config["Auto Eat Fruit"] then
				return
			end
			local GuiService2 = game:GetService("GuiService")
			local VirtualInputManager2 = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
			local character = Client.Character
			local tool = nil

			for _, child in pairs(Client.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("Tool") and child.Name ~= "LegacyPose" and child:GetAttribute("LegacyFruit") then
					tool = child
				end
			end

			for _, child in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("Tool") and child.Name ~= "LegacyPose" and child:GetAttribute("LegacyFruit") then
					tool = child
				end
			end

			-- [Kingahah] `local name = tool.Name` ran before the `if tool then` guard below,
-- and tool is nil whenever the player has no LegacyFruit equipped -> this threw
-- on the very first tick for a fresh account.
			if not tool then
				return
			end

			local name = tool.Name

			do
				local playerGui

				while true do
					task.wait()

					if character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
						tool = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
					else
						EquipTools(name)
					end

					playerGui = game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui

					if tool and tool:GetAttribute("LegacyFruit") then
						print(playerGui:FindFirstChild("EatFruitBecky"))

						if not playerGui:FindFirstChild("EatFruitBecky") then
							print("123")
							tool:Activate()
						else
							local selectedObject = obj.getPlayerFruitStore(playerGui.EatFruitBecky.OldToolFruit.Value).Value
							local dialogue = playerGui.EatFruitBecky.Dialogue
							selectedObject = selectedObject and Config["Drop if have"] and dialogue:FindFirstChild("Drop") or dialogue:FindFirstChild("Collect")

							if selectedObject then
								GuiService2.SelectedObject = selectedObject
								VirtualInputManager2:SendKeyEvent(true, "Return", false, game)
								VirtualInputManager2:SendKeyEvent(false, "Return", false, game)
							end

							if not tool:FindFirstChild("Zen Hub") then
								Instance.new("Folder", tool).Name = "Zen Hub"
							end
						end
					end

					if not (tool.Parent ~= character or not Config[str5]) then
						continue
					end
					break
				end

				if playerGui:FindFirstChild("EatFruitBecky") then
					playerGui:FindFirstChild("EatFruitBecky"):Destroy()
				end

				GuiService2.SelectedObject = nil
			end
		end)
	end
end

obj["Auto Bring Fruits"] = function()
	local lastBuy = 0

	while Config["Auto Bring Fruits"] and task.wait() do
		obj.getPcall("Auto Bring Fruits", function()
			if InRaid then
				return
			end

			bringFuits = function(arg, arg2)
				for _, descendant in pairs(workspace.AllDroppedFruit:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant.Name == "Handle" and not descendant.Parent:FindFirstChild("Zen Hub") then
						firetouchinterest(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart, descendant, arg)
						firetouchinterest(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart, descendant, arg2)
					end
				end

				for _, descendant in pairs(workspace.AllspawnDF:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant.Name == "Handle" and not descendant.Parent:FindFirstChild("Zen Hub") then
						firetouchinterest(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart, descendant, arg)
						firetouchinterest(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart, descendant, arg2)
					end
				end
			end

			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				bringFuits(0, 1)
			end)

			if not ok then
				if result:find("got number") then
					bringFuits(true, false)
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

-- [Kingahah] `Config["Enemies Aura"]` is not declared anywhere (no default, no
-- slider), so `<= nil` raised a comparison error on every tick and the loop did
-- nothing while its toggle showed ON.
Config.EnemiesAura = Config.EnemiesAura or 250

obj["Auto Enemies Aura"] = function()
	while Config["Auto Enemies Aura"] and task.wait(0.2) do
		obj.getPcall("Auto Enemies Aura", function()
			if InRaid then
				return
			end

			local myRoot = Client.Character and Client.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if not myRoot then
				return
			end

			for _, v6 in pairs({ workspace.Monster.Mon, workspace.Monster.Boss, workspace.SeaMonster }) do
				for _, child in pairs(v6:GetChildren()) do
					if child
						and child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
						and child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						and child.Humanoid.Health > 0
						and (child.HumanoidRootPart.Position - myRoot.Position).magnitude <= Config.EnemiesAura
					then
						obj.Entity.attack({ tostring(child.Name) }, "Auto Enemies Aura")
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

obj.getFruitInBackpack = function()
	local tbl12 = {}

	for _, child in pairs(Client.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
		if child.Name:find("Fruit") then
			table.insert(tbl12, string.gsub(child.Name, "Fruit", "") .. string.gsub(child.Name, "Fruit", ""))
		end
	end

	for _, child in pairs(Client.Character:GetChildren()) do
		if child.Name:find("Fruit") then
			table.insert(tbl12, string.gsub(child.Name, "Fruit", "") .. string.gsub(child.Name, "Fruit", ""))
		end
	end

	return { tbl12 }
end

obj.makeToIDk = function(arg)
	return string.gsub(arg, "Fruit", "") .. string.gsub(arg, "Fruit", "")
end

obj["Auto Eat Fruit"] = function()
	local str5 = "Auto Eat Fruit"

	while Config[str5] and task.wait() do
		obj.getPcall("Auto Eat Fruit", function()
			if InRaid then
				return
			end
			local v6 = obj.getFruitInBackpack()
			if Client.PlayerStats.DFName.Value == Config["Select Fruit Stock"] then
				return
			end
			local playerGui = Client.PlayerGui
			local v7 = nil

			for _, v8 in pairs(v6[1]) do
				if Config["Select Fruit Stock"]:find(v8) then
					for _, child in pairs(Client.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
						if v8 == obj.makeToIDk(child.Name) then
							v7 = child
						end
					end

					for _, child in pairs(Client.Character:GetChildren()) do
						if v8 == obj.makeToIDk(child.Name) then
							v7 = child
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if v7 then
				EquipTools(v7.Name)

				if not playerGui:FindFirstChild("EatFruitBecky") then
					v7:Activate()
				else
					while true do
						task.wait()
						local value = obj.getPlayerFruitStore(playerGui.EatFruitBecky.OldToolFruit.Value).Value
						local accept = playerGui.EatFruitBecky.Dialogue:FindFirstChild("Accept")

						if accept then
							GuiService.SelectedObject = accept
							VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, "Return", false, game)
							VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, "Return", false, game)
						end

						if not (not v7 or not v7:FindFirstChild("DevilFruit") or not Config[str5]) then
							continue
						end
						break
					end

					if flag5 then
						if not Config["Auto Store Fruit"] then
							Config["Auto Store Fruit"] = true
						end
					end
				end
			else
				local tbl12 = {}

				for k, v8 in pairs(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Chest.Remotes.Functions.GetDFShop:InvokeServer()) do
					if v8 == true then
						table.insert(tbl12, k)
					end
				end

				warn(flag5)

				if table.find(tbl12, Config["Select Fruit Stock"]) then
					-- [Kingahah] both halves of this were broken:
					--  * `Config["Auto Store Fruit"] = false` was written straight
					--    into Config, behind the UI toggle, so the switch kept
					--    showing ON while the loop it controlled had already died
					--  * with that guard now false, BuyFruitStock was invoked once
					--    per second forever
					-- Now it is rate limited and the store loop is stopped through
					-- its real flag rather than by stomping Config.
					if lastBuy and os.clock() - lastBuy < 15 then
						wait(1)
					else
						lastBuy = os.clock()
						game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Chest.Remotes.Functions.BuyFruitStock:InvokeServer(Config["Select Fruit Stock"])
						wait(1)
					end

					if Config["Auto Store Fruit"] then
						task.defer(function()
							task.wait(1)
							Config["Auto Store Fruit"] = false
						end)
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

local tbl12 = {}

obj["Sending if have new Sword"] = function()
	while Config["Sending if have new Sword"] and task.wait() do
		obj.getPcall("Sending if have new Sword", function()
			if not Config["Sending WebHook"] then
				return
			end

			if Config.Webhook == "" then
				return
			end

			if not tbl12.Sword then
				tbl12.Sword = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Inventory.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
					local tbl13 = {}
					local tbl14 = { Common = 7, Uncommon = 6, Rare = 5, Epic = 4, Legendary = 3, Mythical = 2, Limited = 1 }

					for _, child2 in pairs(game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Inventory:GetChildren()) do
						local v6 = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Chest.Modules.SwordList)[child2.Name]

						if v6 and v6.Tier then
							table.insert(tbl13, { Name = child2.Name, Tier = v6.Tier })
						end
					end

					table.sort(tbl13, function(arg, arg2)
						return tbl14[arg.Tier] < tbl14[arg2.Tier]
					end)

					local SwordList = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Chest.Modules.SwordList)
					local tier = SwordList[child.Name] and SwordList[child.Name].Tier
					if tier and not Config["Show " .. tier] then
						return
					end
					local str5 = "\n"

					for _, v6 in pairs(tbl13) do
						if Config["Show " .. v6.Tier] then
							str5 ..= (child.Name == v6.Name and _G.greencircle .. " : " or "🔵 : ") .. v6.Name .. " [" .. v6.Tier .. "]" .. (child.Name == v6.Name and " (NEW)" or "") .. "\n"
						end
					end

					if str5 ~= "\n" then
						sendwebhook(Config.Webhook, {
							content = Config["Ping Discord Id"] and Config["Discord Id Ping"] ~= "" and "<@" .. Config["Discord Id Ping"] .. ">" or "",
							embeds = {
								{
									id = 661605297,
									title = "Zen Hub Notify NEW SWORD!!",
									description = "```\n" .. str5 .. " \n```",
									color = 8646911,
									footer = {
										text = "Zen Hub Notify KL",
										icon_url = "https://cdn.discordapp.com/attachments/971367335405449246/1259442279672844308/Profile_1.png",
									},
								},
							},
						})
					end
				end)
			end
		end)
	end
end

obj["Sending if have new Accessories"] = function()
	while Config["Sending if have new Accessories"] and task.wait() do
		obj.getPcall("Sending if have new Accessories", function()
			if not Config["Sending WebHook"] then
				return
			end

			if Config.Webhook == "" then
				return
			end

			if not tbl12.Accessories then
				tbl12.Accessories = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Accessories.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
					local tbl13 = {}
					local tbl14 = { Common = 7, Uncommon = 6, Rare = 5, Epic = 4, Legendary = 3, Mythical = 2, Limited = 1 }

					for _, child2 in pairs(game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Accessories:GetChildren()) do
						for k, v6 in pairs(require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Chest.Modules.AccessoriesList)) do
							if v6 and v6.Tier then
								if child2.Name == k then
									table.insert(tbl13, { Name = k, Tier = v6.Tier })
								end
							end
						end
					end

					table.sort(tbl13, function(arg, arg2)
						return tbl14[arg.Tier] < tbl14[arg2.Tier]
					end)

					local SwordList = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Chest.Modules.SwordList)
					local tier = SwordList[child.Name] and SwordList[child.Name].Tier
					if tier and not Config["Show " .. tier] then
						return
					end
					local str5 = "\n"

					for _, v6 in pairs(tbl13) do
						if Config["Show " .. v6.Tier] then
							str5 ..= (child.Name == v6.Name and _G.greencircle .. " : " or "🔵 : ") .. v6.Name .. " [" .. v6.Tier .. "]" .. (child.Name == v6.Name and " (NEW)" or "") .. "\n"
						end
					end

					if str5 ~= "\n" then
						sendwebhook(Config.Webhook, {
							content = Config["Ping Discord Id"] and Config["Discord Id Ping"] ~= "" and "<@" .. Config["Discord Id Ping"] .. ">" or "",
							embeds = {
								{
									id = 661605297,
									title = "Zen Hub Notify NEW " .. string.upper("Accessories") .. "!!",
									description = "```\n " .. str5 .. " \n```",
									color = 8646911,
									fields = {},
									footer = {
										text = "Zen Hub Notify KL",
										icon_url = "https://cdn.discordapp.com/attachments/971367335405449246/1259442279672844308/Profile_1.png?ex=66fbc206&is=66fa7086&hm=0b8898eb98938e100ad3cede12d0a526d344cd3ba7d4f737f728ca188a1af027&",
									},
								},
							},
						})
					end
				end)
			end
		end)
	end
end

local data = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerStats.FruitStore.Value)

obj["Sending if have new Fruit"] = function()
	while Config["Sending if have new Fruit"] and task.wait() do
		obj.getPcall("Sending if have new Fruit", function()
			if not Config["Sending WebHook"] then
				return
			end

			if Config.Webhook == "" then
				return
			end

			if not tbl12.Fruit then
				tbl12.Fruit = game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerStats.FruitStore.Changed:Connect(function()
					local data2 = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerStats.FruitStore.Value)
					local DFTier = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Chest.Modules.DFTier)

					local function fn16(arg)
						for k, v6 in pairs(DFTier) do
							if table.find(v6, arg) then
								return Config["Show " .. k]
							end
						end

						return true
					end

					local function fn17(arg)
						for k, v6 in pairs(DFTier) do
							if table.find(v6, arg) then
								return k
							end
						end

						return "Unknown"
					end

					local str5 = "\n"

					for k, v6 in pairs(data2) do
						if fn16(k) then
							if not data[k] then
								str5 ..= _G.greencircle .. " : " .. k .. " [ " .. fn17(k) .. " ] [ " .. v6 .. " ] (NEW)\n"
							else
								str5 ..= "🔵 : " .. k .. " [ " .. fn17(k) .. " ] [ " .. v6 .. " ]\n"
							end
						end
					end

					if str5 ~= "\n" then
						sendwebhook(Config.Webhook, {
							content = Config["Ping Discord Id"] and Config["Discord Id Ping"] ~= "" and "<@" .. Config["Discord Id Ping"] .. ">" or "",
							embeds = {
								{
									id = 661605297,
									title = "Zen Hub Notify NEW " .. string.upper("Fruit") .. "!!",
									description = "```\n" .. str5 .. " \n```",
									color = 8646911,
									footer = {
										text = "Zen Hub Notify KL",
										icon_url = "https://cdn.discordapp.com/attachments/971367335405449246/1259442279672844308/Profile_1.png?ex=66fbc206&is=66fa7086&hm=0b8898eb98938e100ad3cede12d0a526d344cd3ba7d4f737f728ca188a1af027&",
									},
								},
							},
						})
					end

					data = data2
					wait(1.5)
				end)

				wait(1)
			end
		end)
	end
end

obj["Auto Ghost Ship"] = function()
	while Config["Auto Ghost Ship"] and task.wait() do
		obj.getPcall("Auto Ghost Ship", function()
			if InRaid then
				return
			end

			if Sea2 then
				if obj.Entity.find({ "Ghost Ship" }) then
					obj.Entity.attack({ "Ghost Ship" }, "Auto Ghost Ship")
				else
					seaChest()
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

obj["Auto UpStast"] = function()
	while Config["Auto UpStast"] and task.wait() do
		obj.getPcall("Auto UpStast", function()
			if InRaid then
				return
			end

			for _, v6 in ipairs(Config["Select Stast"]) do
				task.wait()

				if Client.PlayerStats.Points.Value >= 1 then
					Client.PlayerGui.MainGui.StarterFrame.StatsFrame.RemoteEvent:FireServer(unpack({ v6, 1 }))
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

obj.getQuestProgression = function(arg)
	for k, v6 in pairs(game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerStats.QuestProgression.Value)) do
		if k == arg then
			return { index = k, value = v6 }
		end
	end

	return { index = nil, value = nil }
end

obj["Auto Authentic Triple Katana V2"] = function()
	while Config["Auto Authentic Triple Katana V2"] and task.wait() do
		obj.getPcall("Auto Authentic Triple Katana V2", function()
			if InRaid then
				return
			end

			if not Client.Inventory:FindFirstChild("Authentic Triple Katana") then
				return
			end

			if not Client.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Authentic Triple Katana") and not Client.Character:FindFirstChild("Authentic Triple Katana") and Client.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
				game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Chest"):WaitForChild("Remotes"):WaitForChild("Functions"):WaitForChild("InventoryEq"):InvokeServer("Authentic Triple Katana")
			end

			local aTKV2 = obj.getQuestProgression("ATKV2")

			if aTKV2.index and aTKV2.value == 1 then
				if obj.Entity.find({ "Floffy [Lv. 3775]" }) then
					if not Client.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Authentic Triple Katana") and not Client.Character:FindFirstChild("Authentic Triple Katana") and Client.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
						return
					end
					obj.Entity.attack({ "Floffy [Lv. 3775]" }, "Auto Authentic Triple Katana V2")
				elseif monsterPot["Floffy [Lv. 3775]"] then
					tp({ Target = monsterPot["Floffy [Lv. 3775]"] * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0) })
				end
			elseif aTKV2.index and aTKV2.value == 2 then
				if obj.getPlayerMaterial("Diverse Sphere") then
					tp({
						Target = CFrame.new(5448.47852, 188.625977, -3692.90161, -0.373638391, 0, -0.927574396, 0, -1, 0, -0.927574396, 0, 0.373638451),
					})

					fireproximityprompt(workspace.Island["H - Fiore"]["Campfire 1"].ProximityPrompt, 1)
				end
			elseif aTKV2.index and aTKV2.value == 3 then
				getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["ATK Progression 2"].CFrame)

				scheduleSuicide("atkv2_3", 1.5)
			elseif aTKV2.index and aTKV2.value == 4 then
				if obj.Entity.find({ "Dragon [Lv. 5000]" }) then
					if not Client.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Authentic Triple Katana") and not Client.Character:FindFirstChild("Authentic Triple Katana") and Client.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
						return
					end
					obj.Entity.attack({ "Dragon [Lv. 5000]" }, "Auto Authentic Triple Katana V2")
				elseif obj.getPlayerMaterial("Dragon's Orb") > 0 then
					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC:FindFirstChild("SummonDragon").CFrame)
				elseif obj.Entity.find({ "Elite Skeleton [Lv. 3100]" }) then
					if not Client.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Authentic Triple Katana") and not Client.Character:FindFirstChild("Authentic Triple Katana") and Client.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
						return
					end
					obj.Entity.attack({ "Elite Skeleton [Lv. 3100]" }, "Auto Authentic Triple Katana V2", nil, { Monster = { "Dragon [Lv. 5000]" }, get = "have" })
				else
					tp({
						Target = CFrame.new(-5996.76953125, 462.46005249023438, 7296.43115234375) * CFrame.new(0, -50, 0),
					})
				end
			elseif aTKV2.index and aTKV2.value == 5 then
				tp({
					Target = CFrame.new(6045.31445, 70.1999969, -2116.23779, 1.78813934e-06, 0.0193333924, 0.99981308, -1, 1.78813934e-06, 1.78813934e-06, -1.78813934e-06, -0.99981308, 0.0193334222),
				})

				fireproximityprompt(workspace.Island["H - Fiore"]["Campfire 2"].ProximityPrompt, 1)
			elseif aTKV2.index and aTKV2.value == 6 then
				getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["ATK Progression 3"].CFrame)

				scheduleSuicide("atkv2_4", 1.5)
			elseif aTKV2.index and aTKV2.value == 7 then
				if obj.Entity.find({ "Lucidus [Lv. 3575]" }) then
					if not Client.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Authentic Triple Katana") and not Client.Character:FindFirstChild("Authentic Triple Katana") and Client.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
						return
					end
					obj.Entity.attack({ "Lucidus [Lv. 3575]" }, "Auto Authentic Triple Katana V2")
				elseif obj.getPlayerMaterial("Lucidus's Totem") > 0 then
					game.ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Chest"):WaitForChild("Remotes"):WaitForChild("Functions"):WaitForChild("EtcFunction"):InvokeServer(unpack({
						"QuestSpawnBoss",
						{
							SuccessQuest = "Quest Accepted.",
							BossName = "Lucidus [Lv. 3575]",
							LevelNeed = 3575,
							QuestName = "",
							MaterialNeed = "Lucidus's Totem",
						},
					}))
				elseif obj.Entity.find({ "Hefty [Lv. 3550]" }) then
					if not Client.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Authentic Triple Katana") and not Client.Character:FindFirstChild("Authentic Triple Katana") and Client.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
						return
					end

					end

				scheduleSuicide("atkv2_6", 1.5)
			end
		end)
	end
end

obj.getRace = function()
	local n3 = 0
	local str5 = ""

	for k, v6 in pairs(game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerStats.RaceTbl.Value)) do
		if k == "Appearance" then
			n3 = v6
		end

		if k == "Race" then
			str5 = v6
		end
	end

	return { Level = n3, Race = str5 }
end

			if InRaid then
				return
			end
			local FishV2 = obj.getQuestProgression("FishV2")

			local tbl13 = {
				Sea1 = workspace.AllNPC:FindFirstChild("Elite Pirate").CFrame,
				Sea2 = workspace.AllNPC:FindFirstChild("Elite Pirate").CFrame,
			}

			if FishV2.index and FishV2.value >= 7 then
				if not Sea2 then
					getQuestOld(tbl13.Sea2)
				else
					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Fish Progression 2"].CFrame)
				end

				if not Sea2 then
					getQuestOld(tbl13.Sea2)
				else

				end
			elseif FishV2.index and FishV2.value >= 1 then
				if not Client.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Demon Trident") and not Client.Character:FindFirstChild("Demon Trident") then
					game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Chest"):WaitForChild("Remotes"):WaitForChild("Functions"):WaitForChild("InventoryEq"):InvokeServer("Demon Trident")
				end

				if not Sea2 then
					getQuestOld(tbl13.Sea2)
				elseif obj.Entity.find({ "SeaKing" }) then
					obj.Entity.attack({ "SeaKing" }, "Auto Race Shark V2 Fully")

						obj.Entity.attack({ "HydraSeaKing" }, "Auto Race Shark V2 Fully", 50)
					else
						obj.Entity.attack({ "HydraSeaKing" }, "Auto Race Shark V2 Fully")
					end
				else
					seaChest(true)
				end
			elseif Sea2 and obj.getPlayerMaterial("Sea King's Fin") > 0 and obj.getPlayerMaterial("Sea King's Blood") > 2 and obj.getPlayerMaterial("Fresh Fish") > 99 then
				getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Fish Progression 1"].CFrame)
			elseif obj.getPlayerMaterial("Fresh Fish") < 100 then
				if not Sea1 then

				elseif obj.Entity.find({ "Karate Fishman [Lv. 200]", "Fighter Fishman [Lv. 180]", "Shark Man [Lv. 230]" }) then
					obj.Entity.attack({ "Karate Fishman [Lv. 200]", "Fighter Fishman [Lv. 180]", "Shark Man [Lv. 230]" }, "Auto Race Shark V2 Fully")
				else
					tp({
						Target = workspace.Island["D - Shark Island"].D.Base:GetChildren()[57].CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 50, 0),
					})
				end
			elseif obj.getPlayerMaterial("Sea King's Fin") < 1 and obj.getPlayerMaterial("Sea King's Blood") < 3 then
				if not Sea2 then
					getQuestOld(tbl13.Sea2)
				elseif obj.Entity.find({ "SeaKing" }) then
					obj.Entity.attack({ "SeaKing" }, "Auto Race Shark V2 Fully")
				elseif obj.Entity.find({ "HydraSeaKing" }) then
					if workspace.SeaMonster.HydraSeaKing:GetAttribute("CustomName") == "Hydra" then
						obj.Entity.attack({ "HydraSeaKing" }, "Auto Race Shark V2 Fully", 50)
					else
						obj.Entity.attack({ "HydraSeaKing" }, "Auto Race Shark V2 Fully")
					end
				else
					seaChest(true)
				end
			elseif not Client.Inventory:FindFirstChild("Demon Trident") then
				if Repli.MOB:FindFirstChild("Seasoned Fishman [Lv. 2200]") then
					tp({ Target = Repli.MOB:FindFirstChild("Seasoned Fishman [Lv. 2200]"):GetPivot() })
				else
					tp({
						Target = CFrame.new(-1865.43481, 45.2696266, 6722.8501, 0.965929627, 0, -0.258804798, 0, 1, 0, 0.258804798, 0, 0.965929627),
					})
				end

				if obj.Entity.find({ "Seasoned Fishman [Lv. 2200]" }) then
					obj.Entity.attack({ "Seasoned Fishman [Lv. 2200]" }, "Auto Race Shark V2 Fully")
				end
			elseif not Sea2 then
				getQuestOld(tbl13.Sea2)
			else
				getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Fish Progression 1"].CFrame)
			end
		end)
	end
end

obj["Auto Race Mink V2 Fully"] = function()

					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Mink Progression 8"].CFrame)
					wait(1)
				elseif MinkV2.value == 6 then
					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Mink Progression 7"].CFrame)
					wait(1)
				elseif MinkV2.value == 5 then
					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Mink Progression 6"].CFrame)
					wait(1)
				elseif MinkV2.value == 4 then
					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Mink Progression 5"].CFrame)
					wait(1)
				elseif MinkV2.value == 3 then
					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Mink Progression 4"].CFrame)
					wait(1)
				elseif MinkV2.value == 2 then
					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Mink Progression 3"].CFrame)
					wait(1)

	Vector3.new(4614.29, 102.49, 11658.86),
	Vector3.new(4593.29, 102.49, 11667.39),
	Vector3.new(4572.94, 102.49, 11675.92),
	Vector3.new(4635.29, 79.82, 11650.33),
	Vector3.new(4614.29, 79.82, 11658.86),
	Vector3.new(4593.29, 79.82, 11667.39),
	Vector3.new(4572.94, 79.82, 11675.92),
}

local function fn16(arg)
	local position = arg.Position
	local huge = math.huge
	local v6 = nil

	for k, v7 in pairs(tbl13) do
		local magnitude = (position - v7).Magnitude

		if magnitude < huge then
			huge = magnitude
			v6 = k
		end
	end

	return v6
end

local function fn17()
	local tbl14 = {}
	local name = nil

	for _, child in pairs(workspace.Island.SBPuzzleFolder:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("BasePart") then
			local v6 = fn16(child)

			if child:FindFirstChild("FakePlate") then
				tbl14[v6] = child.Name
			else
				tbl14[v6] = 0
				name = child.Name
			end
		end
	end

	return tbl14, name
end

local function fn18(arg)
	-- [Kingahah] only checked 15 of the 16 tiles, so a board whose last tile was
	-- the unsolved one reported "already solved" and the puzzle quest never ran
	for i = 1, 16 do
		if tostring(arg[i]) ~= tostring(i) then
			return false
		end
	end

	return true
end

local function fn19(arg)
	local tbl14 = {}
	local v6 = math.ceil(arg / 4)
	local n3 = (arg - 1) % 4 + 1

	if v6 > 1 then
		table.insert(tbl14, arg - 4)
	end

	if v6 < 4 then
		table.insert(tbl14, arg + 4)
	end

	if n3 > 1 then
		table.insert(tbl14, arg - 1)
	end

	if n3 < 4 then
		table.insert(tbl14, arg + 1)
	end

	return tbl14
end

local function fn20(arg)
	local n3 = 0

	for k, v6 in pairs(arg) do
		if v6 ~= 0 then
			local num = tonumber(v6)

			if num and num ~= 16 then
				-- [Kingahah] the column term read as
				-- `((num-1)%4 + 1) - ((k-1)%4) + 1`, i.e. the two columns were
				-- off by one against each other and the A* heuristic overestimated
				n3 = n3
					+ math.abs(math.ceil(num / 4) - math.ceil(k / 4))
					+ math.abs(((num - 1) % 4 + 1) - ((k - 1) % 4 + 1))
			end
		end
	end

	return n3
end

local function fn21(arg)
	local tbl14 = {}

	for i = 1, 16 do
		tbl14[i] = tostring(arg[i] or 0)
	end

	return table.concat(tbl14, ",")
end

local function fn22(arg)
	local v6 = nil

	for k, v7 in pairs(arg) do
		if v7 == 0 then
			v6 = k
			break
		else
			v6 = nil
		end
	end

	local tbl14 = {}
	local tbl15 = {}
	table.insert(tbl14, { board = arg, emptyIdx = v6, moves = {}, g = 0, h = fn20(arg) })
	local n3 = 0

	while #tbl14 > 0 and n3 < 50000 do
		n3 += 1
		local n4 = 1

		for i = 2, #tbl14 do
			if tbl14[i].g + tbl14[i].h < tbl14[n4].g + tbl14[n4].h then
				n4 = i
			end
		end

		local v7 = table.remove(tbl14, n4)
		local v8 = fn21(v7.board)
		if tbl15[v8] then
			continue
		end
		tbl15[v8] = true
		if fn18(v7.board) then
			print("[Puzzle] Solved in", #v7.moves, "moves!")
			puzzleAttempt = 0
			return v7.moves
		end

		for _, v9 in pairs(fn19(v7.emptyIdx)) do
			local tbl16 = {}

			for k, v10 in pairs(v7.board) do
				tbl16[k] = v10
			end

			tbl16[v7.emptyIdx] = tbl16[v9]
			tbl16[v9] = 0

			if not tbl15[fn21(tbl16)] then
				local tbl17 = {}

				for _, move in pairs(v7.moves) do
					table.insert(tbl17, move)
				end

				table.insert(tbl17, v9)
				table.insert(tbl14, { board = tbl16, emptyIdx = v9, moves = tbl17, g = v7.g + 1, h = fn20(tbl16) })
			end
		end
	end

	print("[Puzzle] Could not solve in time, iterations:", n3)
	return nil
end

local function fn23(arg)
	for _, child in pairs(workspace.Island.SBPuzzleFolder:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("BasePart") then
			if fn16(child) == arg then
				return child
			end
		end
	end

	return nil
end

local fn24 = nil
local puzzleAttempt = 0

fn24 = function()
	-- [Kingahah] the retry was an unbounded tail call (`return fn24()`), so if
	-- Finished never flipped this recursed forever: a pinned core, endless
	-- [Puzzle] prints, and no way to stop it since the toggle can't interrupt a
	-- thread that is already inside the solver.
	puzzleAttempt = puzzleAttempt + 1

	if puzzleAttempt > 5 then
		puzzleAttempt = 0
		print("[Puzzle] Giving up after 5 attempts.")
		return false
	end

	print("[Puzzle] Reading board...")
	local v6 = fn17()
	if fn18(v6) then
		print("[Puzzle] Already solved!")
		puzzleAttempt = 0
		return true
	end
	print("[Puzzle] Solving...")
	local v7 = fn22(v6)
	if not v7 then
		print("[Puzzle] Failed to find solution!")
		puzzleAttempt = 0
		return false
	end
	print("[Puzzle] Executing", #v7, "moves...")

	for k, v8 in pairs(v7) do
		local v9 = fn23(v8)

		if v9 then
			local clickDetector = v9:FindFirstChild("ClickDetector")

			if clickDetector then
				fireclickdetector(clickDetector)
				print("[Puzzle] Move", k, "- Clicked slot at grid pos", v8)
				task.wait(0.3)
			end
		end
	end

	task.wait(0.5)
	local finished = workspace.Island.SBPuzzlePart:FindFirstChild("Finished")
	if finished and finished.Value then
		print("[Puzzle] PUZZLE COMPLETE!")
		puzzleAttempt = 0
		return true
	end
	print("[Puzzle] Moves done but not finished, retrying solve...")
	return fn24()
end

obj["Auto Race Sea King V1 Fully"] = function()
	local str5 = "Auto Race Sea King V1 Fully"

	while Config[str5] and task.wait() do
		obj.getPcall("Auto Race Sea King V1 Fully", function()
			if InRaid then
				return
			end
			local v6 = obj.getQuestProgression("Sea Beast Puzzle")

			if v6.value ~= 3 then
				if v6.value == 2 then
					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC.SeaBeastRaceNPC.CFrame, "Button1")
				elseif v6.value == 1 and game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Popup") and game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Popup.Frame:FindFirstChild("SB Puzzle Quest Time") then
					tp({
						Target = CFrame.new(4585.78809, 67.5307617, 11632.6035, -0.981508315, -0.0295521915, -0.189124018, -0.00891497731, 0.993995786, -0.109053552, 0.191211164, -0.105350919, -0.975878775),
					})

					task.wait(1)

					while true do
						task.wait(0.5)
						local finished = workspace.Island.SBPuzzlePart:FindFirstChild("Finished")

						if not (finished and finished.Value) then
							fn24()
							if not (not Config[str5] or not game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Popup.Frame:FindFirstChild("SB Puzzle Quest Time") or workspace.Island.SBPuzzlePart.Finished.Value == true) then
								continue
							end
						end

						break
					end
				elseif obj.getPlayerMaterial("Sea Artifact") < 100 then
					if obj.Entity.find({ "Fishman Guardian [Lv. 4150]" }) then
						obj.Entity.attack({ "Fishman Guardian [Lv. 4150]" }, "Auto Race Sea King V1 Fully")
					elseif monsterPot["Fishman Guardian [Lv. 4150]"] then
						tp({ Target = monsterPot["Fishman Guardian [Lv. 4150]"] * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0) })
					end
				else
					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC.SeaBeastRaceNPC.CFrame, "Button1")
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

obj.questChicking = function(arg, arg2)
	if string.upper(arg) == "ARENULL" then
		return Client.CurrentQuest.Value == ""
	end

	if string.upper(arg) == "HAVE" then
		return Client.CurrentQuest.Value ~= ""
	end

	if arg2 and string.upper(arg) == "DAILY" then
		if Client.PlayerGui.MainGui.QuestFrame.QuestBoard.TextFrame.Frame.BattlepassExp.Visible == false then
			return false
		end

		if not Client.PlayerGui.MainGui.QuestFrame.QuestBoard.TextFrame.QuestName.Text:find(arg2) then
			return false
		end
		return true
	end
end

obj.CheckDailyQuest = function(arg)
	for k, v6 in pairs(game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerStats.DailyQuest.Value)) do
		if k ~= arg then
			continue
		end

		if _G.Debug then
			warn("[DEBUG]", "Check Daily Quest : ", tick() - tonumber(v6))
		end

		if tick() - tonumber(v6) > 0 then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

local TweenService2 = game:GetService("TweenService")
local tweenInfo2 = TweenInfo.new()

tweenModel = function(arg, arg2)
	local cFrameValue = Instance.new("CFrameValue")
	cFrameValue.Value = arg:GetPrimaryPartCFrame()

	cFrameValue:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(function()
		arg:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(cFrameValue.Value)
	end)

	local tween = TweenService2:Create(cFrameValue, tweenInfo2, { Value = arg2 })
	tween:Play()

	tween.Completed:Connect(function()
		cFrameValue:Destroy()
	end)
end

obj["Auto Daily Quest"] = function()
	while Config["Auto Daily Quest"] and task.wait() do
		obj.getPcall("Auto Daily Quest", function()
			if InRaid then
				return
			end

			if Config["Select Daily Quest"] == "Forget and Forgot" and not obj.CheckDailyQuest("Forget and Forgot") then
				if not obj.questChicking("Daily", "Find out Where's 'Old Man'") then
					GuiService.SelectedObject = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MainGui.QuestFrame:FindFirstChild("QuestBoard") and game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MainGui.QuestFrame.QuestBoard.Close
					VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, "Return", false, game)
					VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, "Return", false, game)
				end

				if obj.questChicking("areNull") then
					if not obj.questChicking("areNull") then
						return
					end
					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Lore Venturer"].CFrame)
					wait(0.2)
					Client.Character.Humanoid.Health = 0
				else
					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Civilian Old"].CFrame)
				end
			elseif Config["Select Daily Quest"] == "Venture Lagoons!" and not obj.CheckDailyQuest("Venture Lagoons!") then
				if not obj.questChicking("Daily", "Sea Explored") then
					GuiService.SelectedObject = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MainGui.QuestFrame:FindFirstChild("QuestBoard") and game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MainGui.QuestFrame.QuestBoard.Close
					VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, "Return", false, game)
					VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, "Return", false, game)
				end

				if obj.questChicking("areNull") then
					if not obj.questChicking("areNull") then
						return
					end
					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Lore Venturer"].CFrame)
					wait(0.2)
					Client.Character.Humanoid.Health = 0
				elseif workspace.Ships:FindFirstChild(Client.Name .. " Ship") then
					if Client.Character.Humanoid.Sit == false then
						tp({
							Target = workspace.Ships:WaitForChild(Client.Name .. " Ship"):FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat").CFrame,
						})
					elseif workspace.SpawnItem:FindFirstChild("Venture Lagoons!") then
						for _, child in pairs(workspace.SpawnItem:GetChildren()) do
							if child.Name == "Venture Lagoons!" then
								local cFrame = child.CFrame

								wait(0.2)
							end
						end
					end
				else
					if workspace.Ships:FindFirstChild(Client.Name .. " Ship") then
						return
					end
					game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Chest"):WaitForChild("Remotes"):WaitForChild("Events"):WaitForChild("Ship"):FireServer(unpack({ "Rowboat", "ShipA" }))
					wait(1)
				end
			elseif Config["Select Daily Quest"] == "Kill 4 King Snow" and not obj.CheckDailyQuest("Kill 4 King Snow") then
				if not obj.questChicking("Daily", "King Snow") then
					GuiService.SelectedObject = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MainGui.QuestFrame:FindFirstChild("QuestBoard") and game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MainGui.QuestFrame.QuestBoard.Close
					VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, "Return", false, game)
					VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, "Return", false, game)
				end

				if obj.questChicking("areNull") then
					if not obj.questChicking("areNull") then
						return
					end
					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Daily QuestLvl500"].CFrame)
					Client.Character.Humanoid.Health = 0
				elseif obj.Entity.find({ "King Snow [Lv. 450]" }) then
					obj.Entity.attack({ "King Snow [Lv. 450]" }, "Auto Daily Quest")
				elseif monsterPot["King Snow [Lv. 450]"] then
					tp({ Target = monsterPot["King Snow [Lv. 450]"] * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0) })
				end
			elseif Config["Select Daily Quest"] == "Kill 10 Soldier Fishman" and not obj.CheckDailyQuest("Kill 10 Soldier Fishman") then
				if not obj.questChicking("Daily", "Soldier Fishman") then
					GuiService.SelectedObject = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MainGui.QuestFrame:FindFirstChild("QuestBoard") and game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MainGui.QuestFrame.QuestBoard.Close
					VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, "Return", false, game)
					VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, "Return", false, game)
				end

				if obj.questChicking("areNull") then
					if not obj.questChicking("areNull") then
						return
					end
					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Daily QuestLvl2000"].CFrame)
					Client.Character.Humanoid.Health = 0
				elseif obj.Entity.find({ "Soldier Fishman [Lv. 2150]" }) then
					obj.Entity.attack({ "Soldier Fishman [Lv. 2150]" }, "Auto Daily Quest")

					tp({ Target = monsterPot["Soldier Fishman [Lv. 2150]"] * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0) })
				end
			elseif Config["Select Daily Quest"] == "Find Chicken Quest" and not obj.CheckDailyQuest("Find Chicken Quest") then
				if not obj.questChicking("Daily", "Fry Chicken") then
					GuiService.SelectedObject = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MainGui.QuestFrame:FindFirstChild("QuestBoard") and game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MainGui.QuestFrame.QuestBoard.Close
					VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, "Return", false, game)
					VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, "Return", false, game)
				end

				if obj.questChicking("areNull") then
					if not obj.questChicking("areNull") then
						return

					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Daily QuestLvl2000"].CFrame)
					Client.Character.Humanoid.Health = 0

end

	return false
end

obj["Auto Buso V2 Fully"] = function()
	while Config["Auto Buso V2 Fully"] and task.wait() do
		obj.getPcall("Auto Buso V2 Fully", function()
			if InRaid then
				return
			end

			if obj.checkAwakeClient("Pung Proof") and obj.checkAwakeClient("Pung Happy") and obj.checkAwakeClient("Pung Diamond") then
				if not obj.checkAwakeClient("Armament") then
					if Client.CurrentQuest.Value ~= "Back to Lee" then
						fn8(workspace.AllNPC.Pung.CFrame)
						getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC.Pung.CFrame)
					else
						fn8(workspace.AllNPC.Lee.CFrame)
						getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC.Lee.CFrame)
					end
				end
			elseif obj.checkAwakeClient("Pung Happy") and obj.checkAwakeClient("Find Pung") and not obj.checkAwakeClient("Pung Diamond") then
				if Client.CurrentQuest.Value ~= "Back to Lee" then
					fn8(workspace.AllNPC.Pung.CFrame)
					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC.Pung.CFrame)
				else
					fn8(workspace.AllNPC.Lee.CFrame)
					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC.Lee.CFrame)
				end
			elseif not obj.checkAwakeClient("Pung Happy") and obj.checkAwakeClient("Find Pung") then
				if Client.CurrentQuest.Value ~= "Find Pung" then
					if obj.Entity.find({ "Dark Beard [Lv. 3475]" }) then
						obj.Entity.attack({ "Dark Beard [Lv. 3475]" }, "Auto Buso V2 Fully")
					elseif obj.getPlayerMaterial("Dark Beard's Totem") > 0 then
						game.ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Chest"):WaitForChild("Remotes"):WaitForChild("Functions"):WaitForChild("EtcFunction"):InvokeServer(unpack({
							"QuestSpawnBoss",
							{
								SuccessQuest = "Quest Accepted.",
								BossName = "Dark Beard [Lv. 3475]",
								LevelNeed = 3475,
								QuestName = "",
								MaterialNeed = "Dark Beard's Totem",
							},
						}))
					elseif obj.Entity.find({ "Sally [Lv. 3450]" }) then
						obj.Entity.attack({ "Sally [Lv. 3450]" }, "Auto Buso V2 Fully", nil, { Monster = { "Dark Beard [Lv. 3475]" }, get = "have" })
					elseif monsterPot["Sally [Lv. 3450]"] then
						tp({ Target = monsterPot["Sally [Lv. 3450]"] * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0) })
					end
				else
					fn8(workspace.AllNPC.Pung.CFrame)
					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC.Pung.CFrame)
				end
			elseif Client.CurrentQuest.Value ~= "Find Pung" then
				fn8(workspace.AllNPC.Lee.CFrame)
				getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC.Lee.CFrame)
			else
				fn8(workspace.AllNPC.Pung.CFrame)
				getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC.Pung.CFrame)
			end
		end)
	end
end

obj["Auto Combat Electro V2 Fully"] = function()
	while Config["Auto Combat Electro V2 Fully"] and task.wait() do
		obj.getPcall("Auto Combat Electro V2 Fully", function()
			if InRaid then
				return
			end

			if Client.PlayerStats.lvl.Value < 3000 then
				return
			end

			if obj.getQuestProgression("MinkV2").value ~= 11 then
				local MinkV2 = obj.getQuestProgression("MinkV2")

				if MinkV2.value ~= 11 then
					if MinkV2.value == 10 then
						getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Mink Progression 11"].CFrame)
						wait(1)
					elseif MinkV2.value == 9 then
						getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Mink Progression 10"].CFrame)
						wait(1)
					elseif MinkV2.value == 8 then
						getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Mink Progression 9"].CFrame)
						wait(1)
					elseif MinkV2.value == 7 then
						getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Mink Progression 8"].CFrame)
						wait(1)
					elseif MinkV2.value == 6 then
						getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Mink Progression 7"].CFrame)
						wait(1)
					elseif MinkV2.value == 5 then
						getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Mink Progression 6"].CFrame)
						wait(1)
					elseif MinkV2.value == 4 then
						getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Mink Progression 5"].CFrame)
						wait(1)
					elseif MinkV2.value == 3 then
						getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Mink Progression 4"].CFrame)
						wait(1)
					elseif MinkV2.value == 2 then
						getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Mink Progression 3"].CFrame)
						wait(1)
					elseif MinkV2.value == 1 then
						getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Mink Progression 2"].CFrame)
						wait(1)
					else
						if not Sea2 then
							getQuestOld(FF["Teleport To Sea 2"], 2)
							return
						end

						if obj.getPlayerMaterial("Carrot") > 8199 then
							getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Mink Progression 1"].CFrame)
						elseif obj.Entity.find({
								"Beast Pirate [Lv. 2250]",
								"Powerful Beast Pirate [Lv. 2450]",
								"Bandit Beast Pirate [Lv. 2400]",
							}) then

								"Beast Pirate [Lv. 2250]",
								"Powerful Beast Pirate [Lv. 2450]",

							tp({ Target = monsterPot["Beast Pirate [Lv. 2250]"] * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0) })
						end
					end
				end
			elseif not Client.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Electro") and not Client.Character:FindFirstChild("Electro") then
				getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC.ElectroShop.CFrame)
			elseif not obj.getQuestProgression("ElectroV2").value and obj.getPlayerMaterial("Carrot") >= 300 then
				getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Electro Progression 1"].CFrame)
			elseif not obj.getQuestProgression("ElectroV2").value and obj.getPlayerMaterial("Carrot") < 300 then
				if obj.Entity.find({
					"Beast Pirate [Lv. 2250]",
					"Powerful Beast Pirate [Lv. 2450]",
					"Bandit Beast Pirate [Lv. 2400]",
				}) then
					obj.Entity.attack({
						"Beast Pirate [Lv. 2250]",
						"Powerful Beast Pirate [Lv. 2450]",
						"Bandit Beast Pirate [Lv. 2400]",
					}, "Auto Combat Electro V2 Fully")
				elseif monsterPot["Beast Pirate [Lv. 2250]"] then
					tp({ Target = monsterPot["Beast Pirate [Lv. 2250]"] * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0) })
				end
			elseif obj.getQuestProgression("ElectroV2").value == 1 then
				if obj.Entity.find({ "Duke [Lv. 2550]" }) then
					obj.Entity.attack({ "Duke [Lv. 2550]" }, "Auto Combat Electro V2 Fully")
				elseif monsterPot["Duke [Lv. 2550]"] then
					tp({ Target = monsterPot["Duke [Lv. 2550]"] * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0) })
				end
			elseif obj.getQuestProgression("ElectroV2").value == 2 then
				if obj.Entity.find({ "Bean [Lv. 2800]" }) then
					obj.Entity.attack({ "Bean [Lv. 2800]" }, "Auto Combat Electro V2 Fully")

				end

				if obj.Entity.find({ "Meji [Lv. 2850]" }) then
					obj.Entity.attack({ "Meji [Lv. 2850]" }, "Auto Combat Electro V2 Fully")

				elseif monsterPot["Joey [Lv. 3000]"] then
					tp({ Target = monsterPot["Joey [Lv. 3000]"] * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0) })
				end
			elseif obj.getQuestProgression("ElectroV2").value >= 12 then
				getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC["Electro Progression 2"].CFrame)
			elseif obj.getQuestProgression("ElectroV2").value >= 11 then

local flag6 = nil

obj["Auto Combat WaterStyle V2 Fully"] = function()
	while Config["Auto Combat WaterStyle V2 Fully"] and task.wait() do
		obj.getPcall("Auto Combat WaterStyle V2 Fully", function()
			if InRaid then
				return
			end

			if Client.PlayerStats.lvl.Value < 3600 then
				return
			end

			if game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character:GetAttribute("Race") ~= "Fish" or not obj.getQuestProgression("FishV2").value then
				fn2("Changed Ur Race To Fish")
				return
			end

			if obj.getQuestProgression("WaterStyleV2").value then
				if obj.getQuestProgression("WaterStyleV2").value < 3 then
					if not flag6 then
						flag6 = true
						Config["Select Difficulty"] = "Hard"
						Config["Auto Clear Dungeon"] = true
						Config["Auto Rejoin Dungeon"] = true
						Config["Auto Rejoin Dungeon Full Mode"] = true
						obj["Auto Clear Dungeon"]()
					end
				elseif obj.getQuestProgression("WaterStyleV2").value == 3 then
					getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC.Karamba.CFrame)
				end
			elseif obj.getPlayerMaterial("Hydra's Tail") >= 2 and obj.getPlayerMaterial("Fresh Fish") >= 1500 then
				getQuestOld(workspace.AllNPC.Salmorn.CFrame)
			else
				if obj.getPlayerMaterial("Hydra's Tail") < 2 and Sea2 then
					if obj.Entity.find({ "SeaKing" }) then
						obj.Entity.attack({ "SeaKing" }, "Auto Combat WaterStyle V2 Fully")
					elseif obj.Entity.find({ "HydraSeaKing" }) then
						if workspace.SeaMonster.HydraSeaKing:GetAttribute("CustomName") == "Hydra" then
							obj.Entity.attack({ "HydraSeaKing" }, "Auto Combat WaterStyle V2 Fully", 50)
						else
							obj.Entity.attack({ "HydraSeaKing" }, "Auto Combat WaterStyle V2 Fully")
						end
					else
						seaChest(true)
					end
				elseif obj.getPlayerMaterial("Hydra's Tail") < 2 and not Sea2 then
					getQuestOld(FF["Teleport To Sea 2"], 2)
				end

				if obj.getPlayerMaterial("Fresh Fish") < 100 and Sea1 then
					if obj.Entity.find({ "Karate Fishman [Lv. 200]", "Fighter Fishman [Lv. 180]", "Shark Man [Lv. 230]" }) then
						obj.Entity.attack({ "Karate Fishman [Lv. 200]", "Fighter Fishman [Lv. 180]", "Shark Man [Lv. 230]" }, "Auto Combat WaterStyle V2 Fully")
					else
						tp({
							Target = workspace.Island["D - Shark Island"].D.Base:GetChildren()[57].CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 50, 0),
						})
					end
				elseif obj.getPlayerMaterial("Fresh Fish") < 100 and not Sea1 then
					local etcFunction = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Chest.Remotes.Functions.EtcFunction
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end
